==================== PI INFRASTRUCTURE ====================
----- C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\infrastructure\announcements\sqlalchemy_publication_execution_repository.py -----
"""SQLAlchemy durable execution repository for WS-22.

Uses the canonical EasyChatBot SQLAlchemy Base. This module deliberately does
not create a second DeclarativeBase/metadata registry.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from uuid import UUID

from sqlalchemy import DateTime, Integer, String, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession, async_sessionmaker
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column

from app.application.announcements.execution.durable_contracts import (
    DurableExecutionStatus,
    PublicationExecutionRecord,
    PublicationExecutionRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.database import Base


class PublicationExecutionRecordModel(Base):
    """Durable operational state for a WS-22 publication execution."""

    __tablename__ = "announcement_publication_executions"

    publication_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(36), nullable=False)
    idempotency_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(255), primary_key=True
    )
    correlation_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(255),
        nullable=False,
    )
    attempt: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer,
        nullable=False,
        default=1,
    )
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(32),
        nullable=False,
    )
    provider_message_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(255),
        nullable=True,
    )
    error_code: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(100),
        nullable=True,
    )
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True),
        nullable=False,
    )
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True),
        nullable=False,
    )


class SqlAlchemyPublicationExecutionRepository(
    PublicationExecutionRepository
):
    """Durable repository using the existing injected async session factory."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        session_factory: async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession],
    ) -> None:
        if session_factory is None:
            raise ValueError("session_factory cannot be None.")

        self._session_factory = session_factory

    async def get_by_idempotency_key(
        self,
        idempotency_key: str,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionRecord | None:
        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            result = await session.execute(
                select(PublicationExecutionRecordModel).where(
                    PublicationExecutionRecordModel.idempotency_key
                    == idempotency_key
                )
            )
            model = result.scalar_one_or_none()

            return self._to_domain(model) if model else None

    async def create_started(
        self,
        *,
        publication_id,
        idempotency_key,
        correlation_id,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionRecord:
        """Create the first durable execution attempt."""

        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

        model = PublicationExecutionRecordModel(
            publication_id=str(publication_id),
            idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
            correlation_id=correlation_id,
            attempt=1,
            status=DurableExecutionStatus.STARTED.value,
            created_at=now,
            updated_at=now,
        )

        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            session.add(model)
            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(model)

            return self._to_domain(model)

    async def mark_succeeded(
        self,
        *,
        idempotency_key,
        provider_message_id,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionRecord:
        """Persist successful completion without changing the attempt count."""

        return await self._update(
            idempotency_key,
            status=DurableExecutionStatus.SUCCEEDED.value,
            provider_message_id=provider_message_id,
            error_code=None,
        )

    async def mark_failed(
        self,
        *,
        idempotency_key,
        error_code,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionRecord:
        """Persist failure while retaining the current attempt number."""

        return await self._update(
            idempotency_key,
            status=DurableExecutionStatus.FAILED.value,
            error_code=error_code,
        )

    async def start_retry(
        self,
        *,
        idempotency_key: str,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionRecord:
        """Increment the durable attempt counter for a retry.

        A retry is valid only for an existing FAILED execution. The same
        idempotency record is retained; no second database row is created.
        """

        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            result = await session.execute(
                select(PublicationExecutionRecordModel).where(
                    PublicationExecutionRecordModel.idempotency_key
                    == idempotency_key
                )
            )

            model = result.scalar_one()

            if model.status != DurableExecutionStatus.FAILED.value:
                raise ValueError(
                    "Only failed executions can be retried."
                )

            model.attempt += 1
            model.status = DurableExecutionStatus.STARTED.value
            model.error_code = None
            model.provider_message_id = None
            model.updated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(model)

            return self._to_domain(model)

    async def _update(
        self,
        idempotency_key,
        **values,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionRecord:
        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            result = await session.execute(
                select(PublicationExecutionRecordModel).where(
                    PublicationExecutionRecordModel.idempotency_key
                    == idempotency_key
                )
            )

            model = result.scalar_one()

            for key, value in values.items():
                setattr(model, key, value)

            model.updated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(model)

            return self._to_domain(model)

    @staticmethod
    def _to_domain(
        model: PublicationExecutionRecordModel,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionRecord:
        return PublicationExecutionRecord(
            publication_id=UUID(model.publication_id),
            idempotency_key=model.idempotency_key,
            correlation_id=model.correlation_id,
            attempt=model.attempt,
            status=DurableExecutionStatus(model.status),
            provider_message_id=model.provider_message_id,
            error_code=model.error_code,
            created_at=model.created_at,
            updated_at=model.updated_at,
        )
----- C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\infrastructure\announcements\sqlalchemy_publication_repository.py -----
"""PostgreSQL adapter for the existing PublicationRepository contract."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone

from sqlalchemy import DateTime, Integer, String, Text, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession, async_sessionmaker
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column

from app.application.announcements.repositories import PublicationRepository
from app.domain.announcements.publication import Publication
from app.domain.announcements.template_models import (
    PublicationStatus,
    PublicationType,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.database import Base


class PublicationModel(Base):
    """Canonical durable announcement publication state."""

    __tablename__ = "announcement_publications"

    publication_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(255), primary_key=True)
    template_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(255), nullable=False)
    template_version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False)
    publication_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)
    title: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120), nullable=False)
    content: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=False)
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False, index=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False)
    approved_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    published_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    failure_reason: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(Text)


class SqlAlchemyPublicationRepository(PublicationRepository):
    """Async PostgreSQL adapter for the existing repository contract."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        session_factory: async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession],
    ) -> None:
        if session_factory is None:
            raise ValueError("session_factory cannot be None.")
        self._session_factory = session_factory

    def _to_domain(self, model: PublicationModel) -> Publication:
        return Publication(
            publication_id=model.publication_id,
            template_id=model.template_id,
            template_version=model.template_version,
            publication_type=PublicationType(model.publication_type),
            title=model.title,
            content=model.content,
            status=PublicationStatus(model.status),
            created_at=model.created_at,
            approved_at=model.approved_at,
            published_at=model.published_at,
            failure_reason=model.failure_reason,
        )

    def _from_domain(self, publication: Publication) -> PublicationModel:
        return PublicationModel(
            publication_id=publication.publication_id,
            template_id=publication.template_id,
            template_version=publication.template_version,
            publication_type=publication.publication_type.value,
            title=publication.title,
            content=publication.content,
            status=publication.status.value,
            created_at=publication.created_at or datetime.now(timezone.utc),
            approved_at=publication.approved_at,
            published_at=publication.published_at,
            failure_reason=publication.failure_reason,
        )

    async def save_async(self, publication: Publication) -> None:
        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            model = await session.get(
                PublicationModel,
                publication.publication_id,
            )
            if model is None:
                session.add(self._from_domain(publication))
            else:
                model.template_id = publication.template_id
                model.template_version = publication.template_version
                model.publication_type = publication.publication_type.value
                model.title = publication.title
                model.content = publication.content
                model.status = publication.status.value
                model.created_at = publication.created_at or model.created_at
                model.approved_at = publication.approved_at
                model.published_at = publication.published_at
                model.failure_reason = publication.failure_reason
            await session.commit()

    async def get_async(self, publication_id: str) -> Publication | None:
        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            model = await session.get(PublicationModel, publication_id)
            return self._to_domain(model) if model else None

    async def list_all_async(self) -> tuple[Publication, ...]:
        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            result = await session.execute(
                select(PublicationModel).order_by(PublicationModel.created_at)
            )
            return tuple(self._to_domain(model) for model in result.scalars().all())

    # The existing repository contract is synchronous. These methods make
    # accidental async DB use explicit rather than silently blocking.
    def save(self, publication: Publication) -> None:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "SqlAlchemyPublicationRepository requires its async boundary; "
            "use save_async()."
        )

    def get(self, publication_id: str) -> Publication | None:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "SqlAlchemyPublicationRepository requires its async boundary; "
            "use get_async()."
        )

    def list_all(self) -> tuple[Publication, ...]:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "SqlAlchemyPublicationRepository requires its async boundary; "
            "use list_all_async()."
        )

----- C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\infrastructure\email\platform_intelligence_escalation_email_sender.py -----
"""Infrastructure adapter for Platform Intelligence escalation email delivery."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_email import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationEmailRequest,
)
from cybersecurity.email.email_configuration import (
    PLATFORM_INTELLIGENCE_EMAIL_FROM,
    PLATFORM_INTELLIGENCE_ESCALATION_RECIPIENTS,
)
from cybersecurity.email.email_provider_manager import EmailProviderManager
from cybersecurity.email.resend_email_provider import ResendEmailProvider


class ResendPlatformIntelligenceEscalationEmailSender:
    """Translate governed PI email requests into the shared email provider."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        provider_manager: EmailProviderManager | None = None,
        recipients: dict[str, str] | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self._recipients = dict(
            recipients
            if recipients is not None
            else PLATFORM_INTELLIGENCE_ESCALATION_RECIPIENTS
        )

        self._provider_manager = (
            provider_manager
            if provider_manager is not None
            else self._build_provider_manager()
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _build_provider_manager() -> EmailProviderManager:
        sender = PLATFORM_INTELLIGENCE_EMAIL_FROM.strip()

        if not sender:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "PLATFORM_INTELLIGENCE_EMAIL_FROM is not configured."
            )

        return EmailProviderManager(
            provider=ResendEmailProvider(
                sender=sender,
            ),
        )

    def send(
        self,
        request: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationEmailRequest,
        *,
        subject: str,
        body: str,
        html_body: str,
    ) -> object:
        if request is None:
            raise ValueError("request cannot be None.")

        recipient = self._recipients.get(request.route)

        if not recipient or not recipient.strip():
            raise RuntimeError(
                "No configured recipient for escalation route: "
                f"{request.route}"
            )

        if not subject.strip():
            raise ValueError("Email subject cannot be empty.")

        if not body.strip():
            raise ValueError("Email body cannot be empty.")

        if not html_body.strip():
            raise ValueError("HTML email body cannot be empty.")

        content: dict[str, Any] = {
            "recipient": recipient.strip(),
            "body": body,
            "html": html_body,
            "email_type": "platform_intelligence_escalation",
            "recommendation_id": request.recommendation_id,
            "risk_decision_id": request.risk_decision_id,
            "review_item_id": request.review_item_id,
            "escalation_route": request.route,
        }

        return self._provider_manager.send(
            subject.strip(),
            content,
        )


__all__ = [
    "ResendPlatformIntelligenceEscalationEmailSender",
]
----- C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\infrastructure\operations_center\sqlalchemy_platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery_audit_repository.py -----
"""PostgreSQL read/append adapter for PI delivery audit history."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping
from typing import Any
from uuid import UUID

from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession, async_sessionmaker

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery_audit import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRecord,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRepository,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus,
)


class SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRepository(
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRepository
):
    _TABLE = "platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery_audit"

    def __init__(self, session_factory: async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession]) -> None:
        if session_factory is None:
            raise ValueError("session_factory cannot be None.")
        self._session_factory = session_factory

    async def append(self, record: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRecord) -> None:
        if record.occurred_at is None:
            raise ValueError("occurred_at is required for durable audit persistence.")
        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            await self._insert(session, record)
            await session.commit()

    async def list_by_notification_key(self, notification_key: str):
        if not notification_key.strip():
            raise ValueError("notification_key must be nonblank.")
        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            result = await session.execute(
                text(
                    f"""
                    SELECT audit_id, notification_key, recommendation_id,
                           risk_decision_id, review_item_id, correlation_id,
                           attempt, status, provider_message_id, error_code,
                           occurred_at
                    FROM {self._TABLE}
                    WHERE notification_key = :notification_key
                    ORDER BY occurred_at ASC, audit_id ASC
                    """
                ),
                {"notification_key": notification_key},
            )
            return tuple(self._to_record(row) for row in result.mappings().all())

    @classmethod
    async def insert_with_session(
        cls,
        session: AsyncSession,
        record: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRecord,
    ) -> None:
        if record.occurred_at is None:
            raise ValueError("occurred_at is required for durable audit persistence.")
        await cls._insert(session, record)

    @classmethod
    async def _insert(
        cls,
        session: AsyncSession,
        record: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRecord,
    ) -> None:
        await session.execute(
            text(
                f"""
                INSERT INTO {cls._TABLE} (
                    audit_id, notification_key, recommendation_id,
                    risk_decision_id, review_item_id, correlation_id,
                    attempt, status, provider_message_id, error_code,
                    occurred_at
                )
                VALUES (
                    :audit_id, :notification_key, :recommendation_id,
                    :risk_decision_id, :review_item_id, :correlation_id,
                    :attempt, :status, :provider_message_id, :error_code,
                    :occurred_at
                )
                """
            ),
            {
                "audit_id": record.audit_id,
                "notification_key": record.notification_key,
                "recommendation_id": record.recommendation_id,
                "risk_decision_id": record.risk_decision_id,
                "review_item_id": record.review_item_id,
                "correlation_id": record.correlation_id,
                "attempt": record.attempt,
                "status": record.status.value,
                "provider_message_id": record.provider_message_id,
                "error_code": record.error_code,
                "occurred_at": record.occurred_at,
            },
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _to_record(row: Mapping[str, Any]) -> PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRecord:
        return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRecord(
            audit_id=UUID(str(row["audit_id"])),
            notification_key=str(row["notification_key"]),
            recommendation_id=str(row["recommendation_id"]),
            risk_decision_id=str(row["risk_decision_id"]),
            review_item_id=str(row["review_item_id"]),
            correlation_id=str(row["correlation_id"]),
            attempt=int(row["attempt"]),
            status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus(str(row["status"])),
            provider_message_id=row["provider_message_id"],
            error_code=row["error_code"],
            occurred_at=row["occurred_at"],
        )
----- C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\infrastructure\operations_center\sqlalchemy_platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery_repository.py -----
"""PostgreSQL persistence adapter for PI escalation delivery."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any
from uuid import uuid4

from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession, async_sessionmaker

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRecord,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRepository,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery_audit import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRecord,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus,
)
from app.infrastructure.operations_center.sqlalchemy_platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery_audit_repository import (
    SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRepository,
)


class SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRepository(
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRepository
):
    _TABLE = "platform_intelligence_escalation_deliveries"

    def __init__(self, session_factory: async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession]) -> None:
        if session_factory is None:
            raise ValueError("session_factory cannot be None.")
        self._session_factory = session_factory

    async def get_by_notification_key(self, notification_key: str):
        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            result = await session.execute(
                text(
                    f"""
                    SELECT notification_key, recommendation_id,
                           risk_decision_id, review_item_id, correlation_id,
                           attempt, status, provider_message_id, error_code,
                           created_at, updated_at
                    FROM {self._TABLE}
                    WHERE notification_key = :notification_key
                    """
                ),
                {"notification_key": notification_key},
            )
            row = result.mappings().first()
            return None if row is None else self._to_record(row)

    async def list_retry_candidates(self, *, limit: int):
        if limit < 1:
            raise ValueError("limit must be at least 1.")
        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            result = await session.execute(
                text(
                    f"""
                    SELECT notification_key, recommendation_id,
                           risk_decision_id, review_item_id, correlation_id,
                           attempt, status, provider_message_id, error_code,
                           created_at, updated_at
                    FROM {self._TABLE}
                    WHERE status IN (:failed, :started)
                    ORDER BY updated_at ASC NULLS FIRST, notification_key ASC
                    LIMIT :limit
                    """
                ),
                {
                    "failed": PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED.value,
                    "started": PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED.value,
                    "limit": limit,
                },
            )
            return tuple(self._to_record(row) for row in result.mappings().all())

    async def claim_retry_candidate(
        self, *, notification_key: str, claim_token: str,
        claimed_at: datetime, lease_expires_at: datetime,
    ) -> bool:
        if not notification_key.strip():
            raise ValueError("notification_key must be nonblank.")
        if not claim_token.strip():
            raise ValueError("claim_token must be nonblank.")
        if claimed_at.tzinfo is None or claimed_at.utcoffset() is None:
            raise ValueError("claimed_at must be timezone-aware.")
        if lease_expires_at.tzinfo is None or lease_expires_at.utcoffset() is None:
            raise ValueError("lease_expires_at must be timezone-aware.")
        if lease_expires_at <= claimed_at:
            raise ValueError("lease_expires_at must be after claimed_at.")

        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            result = await session.execute(
                text(
                    f"""
                    UPDATE {self._TABLE}
                    SET claim_token = :claim_token,
                        claimed_at = :claimed_at,
                        lease_expires_at = :lease_expires_at
                    WHERE notification_key = :notification_key
                      AND status IN (:failed, :started)
                      AND (
                          lease_expires_at IS NULL
                          OR lease_expires_at <= :claimed_at
                      )
                    """
                ),
                {
                    "notification_key": notification_key,
                    "claim_token": claim_token,
                    "claimed_at": claimed_at,
                    "lease_expires_at": lease_expires_at,
                    "failed": PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED.value,
                    "started": PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED.value,
                },
            )
            await session.commit()
            return result.rowcount == 1

    async def create_started(
        self, *, notification_key: str, recommendation_id: str,
        risk_decision_id: str, review_item_id: str, correlation_id: str,
    ):
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            await session.execute(
                text(
                    f"""
                    INSERT INTO {self._TABLE} (
                        notification_key, recommendation_id, risk_decision_id,
                        review_item_id, correlation_id, attempt, status,
                        provider_message_id, error_code, created_at, updated_at,
                        claim_token, claimed_at, lease_expires_at
                    )
                    VALUES (
                        :notification_key, :recommendation_id, :risk_decision_id,
                        :review_item_id, :correlation_id, 1, :status,
                        NULL, NULL, :created_at, :updated_at,
                        NULL, NULL, NULL
                    )
                    """
                ),
                {
                    "notification_key": notification_key,
                    "recommendation_id": recommendation_id,
                    "risk_decision_id": risk_decision_id,
                    "review_item_id": review_item_id,
                    "correlation_id": correlation_id,
                    "status": PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED.value,
                    "created_at": now,
                    "updated_at": now,
                },
            )
            await self._insert_audit(
                session,
                notification_key=notification_key,
                recommendation_id=recommendation_id,
                risk_decision_id=risk_decision_id,
                review_item_id=review_item_id,
                correlation_id=correlation_id,
                attempt=1,
                status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus.STARTED,
                occurred_at=now,
            )
            await session.commit()

        record = await self.get_by_notification_key(notification_key)
        if record is None:
            raise RuntimeError("Created PI escalation delivery could not be reloaded.")
        return record

    async def mark_succeeded(
        self, *, notification_key: str, provider_message_id: str | None,
    ):
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            result = await session.execute(
                text(
                    f"""
                    UPDATE {self._TABLE}
                    SET status = :status,
                        provider_message_id = :provider_message_id,
                        error_code = NULL,
                        updated_at = :updated_at,
                        claim_token = NULL,
                        claimed_at = NULL,
                        lease_expires_at = NULL
                    WHERE notification_key = :notification_key
                    RETURNING notification_key, recommendation_id,
                              risk_decision_id, review_item_id, correlation_id,
                              attempt, status, provider_message_id,
                              error_code, created_at, updated_at
                    """
                ),
                {
                    "notification_key": notification_key,
                    "status": PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.SUCCEEDED.value,
                    "provider_message_id": provider_message_id,
                    "updated_at": now,
                },
            )
            row = result.mappings().first()
            if row is None:
                await session.rollback()
                raise KeyError(f"Unknown PI escalation notification key: {notification_key}")

            record = self._to_record(row)
            if not provider_message_id:
                await session.rollback()
                raise ValueError("Successful PI delivery requires provider_message_id.")

            await self._insert_audit(
                session,
                notification_key=record.notification_key,
                recommendation_id=record.recommendation_id,
                risk_decision_id=record.risk_decision_id,
                review_item_id=record.review_item_id,
                correlation_id=record.correlation_id,
                attempt=record.attempt,
                status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus.SUCCEEDED,
                provider_message_id=provider_message_id,
                occurred_at=now,
            )
            await session.commit()
            return record

    async def mark_failed(self, *, notification_key: str, error_code: str):
        if not error_code.strip():
            raise ValueError("error_code must be nonblank.")
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            result = await session.execute(
                text(
                    f"""
                    UPDATE {self._TABLE}
                    SET status = :status,
                        provider_message_id = NULL,
                        error_code = :error_code,
                        updated_at = :updated_at,
                        claim_token = NULL,
                        claimed_at = NULL,
                        lease_expires_at = NULL
                    WHERE notification_key = :notification_key
                    RETURNING notification_key, recommendation_id,
                              risk_decision_id, review_item_id, correlation_id,
                              attempt, status, provider_message_id,
                              error_code, created_at, updated_at
                    """
                ),
                {
                    "notification_key": notification_key,
                    "status": PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED.value,
                    "error_code": error_code,
                    "updated_at": now,
                },
            )
            row = result.mappings().first()
            if row is None:
                await session.rollback()
                raise KeyError(f"Unknown PI escalation notification key: {notification_key}")

            record = self._to_record(row)
            await self._insert_audit(
                session,
                notification_key=record.notification_key,
                recommendation_id=record.recommendation_id,
                risk_decision_id=record.risk_decision_id,
                review_item_id=record.review_item_id,
                correlation_id=record.correlation_id,
                attempt=record.attempt,
                status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus.FAILED,
                error_code=error_code,
                occurred_at=now,
            )
            await session.commit()
            return record

    async def start_retry(self, *, notification_key: str):
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            result = await session.execute(
                text(
                    f"""
                    UPDATE {self._TABLE}
                    SET attempt = attempt + 1,
                        status = :started,
                        provider_message_id = NULL,
                        error_code = NULL,
                        updated_at = :updated_at,
                        claim_token = NULL,
                        claimed_at = NULL,
                        lease_expires_at = NULL
                    WHERE notification_key = :notification_key
                      AND status = :failed
                    RETURNING notification_key, recommendation_id,
                              risk_decision_id, review_item_id, correlation_id,
                              attempt, status, provider_message_id,
                              error_code, created_at, updated_at
                    """
                ),
                {
                    "notification_key": notification_key,
                    "started": PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED.value,
                    "failed": PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED.value,
                    "updated_at": now,
                },
            )
            row = result.mappings().first()
            if row is None:
                await session.rollback()
                raise RuntimeError(
                    "PI escalation delivery can only be retried from FAILED."
                )

            record = self._to_record(row)
            await self._insert_audit(
                session,
                notification_key=record.notification_key,
                recommendation_id=record.recommendation_id,
                risk_decision_id=record.risk_decision_id,
                review_item_id=record.review_item_id,
                correlation_id=record.correlation_id,
                attempt=record.attempt,
                status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus.STARTED,
                occurred_at=now,
            )
            await session.commit()
            return record

    @staticmethod
    async def _insert_audit(
        session: AsyncSession,
        *,
        notification_key: str,
        recommendation_id: str,
        risk_decision_id: str,
        review_item_id: str,
        correlation_id: str,
        attempt: int,
        status: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus,
        occurred_at: datetime,
        provider_message_id: str | None = None,
        error_code: str | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        record = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRecord(
            audit_id=uuid4(),
            notification_key=notification_key,
            recommendation_id=recommendation_id,
            risk_decision_id=risk_decision_id,
            review_item_id=review_item_id,
            correlation_id=correlation_id,
            attempt=attempt,
            status=status,
            provider_message_id=provider_message_id,
            error_code=error_code,
            occurred_at=occurred_at,
        )
        await SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRepository.insert_with_session(
            session, record
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _to_record(row: Mapping[str, Any]):
        return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRecord(
            notification_key=str(row["notification_key"]),
            recommendation_id=str(row["recommendation_id"]),
            risk_decision_id=str(row["risk_decision_id"]),
            review_item_id=str(row["review_item_id"]),
            correlation_id=str(row["correlation_id"]),
            attempt=int(row["attempt"]),
            status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus(str(row["status"])),
            provider_message_id=row["provider_message_id"],
            error_code=row["error_code"],
            created_at=row["created_at"],
            updated_at=row["updated_at"],
        )

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Mapping


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TemplateEditorField:
    """UI-safe representation of one template input."""

    name: str
    required: bool
    label: str
    multiline: bool = False


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TemplateEditorOption:
    """UI-safe approved template option."""

    template_id: str
    name: str
    category: str
    description: str
    version: int
    publication_type: str
    fields: tuple[TemplateEditorField, ...]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TemplateEditorPreview:
    """Preview result returned without creating or publishing a publication."""

    template_id: str
    template_version: int
    publication_type: str
    content: str


def field_label(name: str) -> str:
    """Convert a controlled field identifier into an operator-friendly label."""
    return name.replace("_", " ").strip().title()


def is_multiline_field(name: str) -> bool:
    """Keep long-form announcement fields in a textarea."""
    return name.lower() in {
        "body",
        "content",
        "summary",
        "description",
        "details",
        "message",
        "changes",
    }


def build_editor_fields(
    required_fields: frozenset[str],
    optional_fields: frozenset[str],
) -> tuple[TemplateEditorField, ...]:
    names = sorted(set(required_fields) | set(optional_fields))
    required = set(required_fields)

    return tuple(
        TemplateEditorField(
            name=name,
            required=name in required,
            label=field_label(name),
            multiline=is_multiline_field(name),
        )
        for name in names
    )

    """
    Domain model representing a Beta user.

    Identity flow:

        beta_users
             |
             v
        telegram_bindings
             |
             v
        Telegram private chat
    """

    user_id: str

    email: str

    email_verified: bool

    beta_approved: bool

    telegram_id: int | None = None

    active_world_id: str | None = None


    @property
    def eligible(self) -> bool:
        """
        User can access Beta if approved and verified.
        """

        return (
            self.email_verified
            and self.beta_approved
        )


    @property
    def active(self) -> bool:
        """
        User can enter private Telegram classroom.
        """

        return (
            self.eligible
            and self.telegram_id is not None
            and self.active_world_id is not None
        )

        DashboardDependencyStatus,
        ...,
    ] = ()

        DashboardIncidentSummary()
    )
    metrics: DashboardOperationalMetrics = (
        DashboardOperationalMetrics()
    )

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "surface": self.surface.value,
            "status": self.status.value,
            "dependencies": [
                dependency.as_dict()
                for dependency in self.dependencies
            ],
            "incidents": self.incidents.as_dict(),
            "metrics": self.metrics.as_dict(),
        }


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DashboardIncident:
    """
    Sanitized incident representation for the dashboard.

    Operational references are retained for authorized investigation.
    Learner content and secrets are never part of this model.
    """

    incident_id: str
    correlation_id: str
    surface: OperationalSurface
    severity: str
    status: str
    first_seen: datetime
    last_seen: datetime
    occurrence_count: int
    user_reference: str | None = None
    world_id: str | None = None

    @classmethod
    def from_incident(
        cls,
        incident: OperationalIncident,
    ) -> "DashboardIncident":
        return cls(
            incident_id=incident.incident_id,
            correlation_id=incident.correlation_id,
            surface=incident.surface,
            severity=incident.severity.value,
            status=incident.status.value,
            first_seen=incident.first_seen,
            last_seen=incident.last_seen,
            occurrence_count=incident.occurrence_count,
            user_reference=incident.user_reference,
            world_id=incident.world_id,
        )

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "incident_id": self.incident_id,
            "correlation_id": self.correlation_id,
            "surface": self.surface.value,
            "severity": self.severity,
            "status": self.status,
            "first_seen": self.first_seen.isoformat(),
            "last_seen": self.last_seen.isoformat(),
            "occurrence_count": self.occurrence_count,
            "user_reference": self.user_reference,
            "world_id": self.world_id,
        }

    ] = ()

            ),
            "announcement_group": (
                self.announcement_group.as_dict()
            ),
            "incidents": [
                incident.as_dict()
                for incident in self.incidents
            ],
        }

----- C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\operations_center\email_workflows\domain.py -----
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from enum import StrEnum


class EmailWorkflowType(StrEnum):
    SCHEDULED = "SCHEDULED"
    EVENT_DRIVEN = "EVENT_DRIVEN"
    MANUAL = "MANUAL"
    SYSTEM = "SYSTEM"


class EmailWorkflowLifecycle(StrEnum):
    ACTIVE = "ACTIVE"
    PAUSED = "PAUSED"
    DISABLED = "DISABLED"
    ARCHIVED = "ARCHIVED"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class EmailWorkflowDefinition:
    workflow_id: str
    name: str
    description: str
    workflow_type: EmailWorkflowType
    lifecycle: EmailWorkflowLifecycle
    owner: str
    trigger: str
    schedule: str | None
    template_id: str
    version: int
    created_at: datetime
    updated_at: datetime

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not self.workflow_id.strip():
            raise ValueError("workflow_id must not be empty")
        if not self.name.strip():
            raise ValueError("name must not be empty")
        if not self.owner.strip():
            raise ValueError("owner must not be empty")
        if not self.trigger.strip():
            raise ValueError("trigger must not be empty")
        if not self.template_id.strip():
            raise ValueError("template_id must not be empty")
        if self.version < 1:
            raise ValueError("version must be >= 1")

        if (
            self.workflow_type is EmailWorkflowType.SCHEDULED
            and not self.schedule
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "SCHEDULED workflows must define a schedule"
            )

        if (
            self.workflow_type is not EmailWorkflowType.SCHEDULED
            and self.schedule is not None
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Only SCHEDULED workflows may define a schedule"
            )

    def can_pause(self) -> bool:
        return (
            self.lifecycle is EmailWorkflowLifecycle.ACTIVE
            and self.workflow_type is EmailWorkflowType.SCHEDULED
        )

    def can_resume(self) -> bool:
        return self.lifecycle is EmailWorkflowLifecycle.PAUSED

    def can_disable(self) -> bool:
        return self.lifecycle in {
            EmailWorkflowLifecycle.ACTIVE,
            EmailWorkflowLifecycle.PAUSED,
        }

    def can_enable(self) -> bool:
        return self.lifecycle is EmailWorkflowLifecycle.DISABLED

    def can_archive(self) -> bool:
        return self.lifecycle is not EmailWorkflowLifecycle.ARCHIVED

    def with_lifecycle(
        self,
        lifecycle: EmailWorkflowLifecycle,
    ) -> EmailWorkflowDefinition:
        return EmailWorkflowDefinition(
            workflow_id=self.workflow_id,
            name=self.name,
            description=self.description,
            workflow_type=self.workflow_type,
            lifecycle=lifecycle,
            owner=self.owner,
            trigger=self.trigger,
            schedule=self.schedule,
            template_id=self.template_id,
            version=self.version + 1,
            created_at=self.created_at,
            updated_at=self.updated_at,
        )

----- C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\private_chat\models.py -----
"""Application models for authenticated World private chat.

These models define the application-layer contracts used by the
PrivateChatService and its repository. They intentionally contain no
infrastructure or provider-specific behaviour.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from uuid import UUID


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class LearningWorldCatalogueEntry:
    """Application representation of an authoritative Learning World."""

    id: UUID
    identifier: str
    name: str
    description: str
    language: str
    version: str
    status: str
    enabled: bool
    world_type: str
    learning_domain: str
    runtime_world_id: str | None

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not isinstance(self.id, UUID):
            raise ValueError("Learning World catalogue id must be a UUID.")

        for field_name in (
            "identifier",
            "name",
            "version",
            "status",
            "world_type",
            "learning_domain",
        ):
            value = getattr(self, field_name)

            if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip():
                raise ValueError(
                    f"Learning World catalogue {field_name} cannot be empty."
                )

        if not isinstance(self.description, str):
            raise ValueError(
                "Learning World catalogue description must be a string."
            )

        if not isinstance(self.language, str) or not self.language.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Learning World catalogue language cannot be empty."
            )

        if self.runtime_world_id is not None:
            if (
                not isinstance(self.runtime_world_id, str)
                or not self.runtime_world_id.strip()
            ):
                raise ValueError(
                    "runtime_world_id cannot be blank when provided."
                )


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PrivateChatConversation:
    """Application representation of a private chat conversation."""

    conversation_id: UUID
    user_id: str
    world_id: str
    created_at: datetime
    updated_at: datetime

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not isinstance(self.conversation_id, UUID):
            raise ValueError("conversation_id must be a UUID.")

        if not isinstance(self.user_id, str) or not self.user_id.strip():
            raise ValueError("user_id cannot be empty.")

        if not isinstance(self.world_id, str) or not self.world_id.strip():
            raise ValueError("world_id cannot be empty.")

        if not isinstance(self.created_at, datetime):
            raise ValueError("created_at must be a datetime.")

        if not isinstance(self.updated_at, datetime):
            raise ValueError("updated_at must be a datetime.")


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PrivateChatMessage:
    """Application representation of a private chat message."""

    conversation_id: UUID
    role: str
    content: str
    created_at: datetime

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not isinstance(self.conversation_id, UUID):
            raise ValueError("conversation_id must be a UUID.")

        if not isinstance(self.role, str) or not self.role.strip():
            raise ValueError("role cannot be empty.")

        if not isinstance(self.content, str) or not self.content.strip():
            raise ValueError("content cannot be empty.")

        if not isinstance(self.created_at, datetime):
            raise ValueError("created_at must be a datetime.")


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PrivateChatActiveConversation:
    """Application representation of the active private chat state."""

    conversation: PrivateChatConversation
    messages: list[PrivateChatMessage]

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not isinstance(self.conversation, PrivateChatConversation):
            raise ValueError(
                "conversation must be a PrivateChatConversation."
            )

        if not isinstance(self.messages, list):
            raise ValueError("messages must be a list.")

        if not all(
            isinstance(message, PrivateChatMessage)
            for message in self.messages
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "messages must contain only PrivateChatMessage instances."
            )


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PrivateChatResponse:
    """Application response returned after processing a private chat message."""

    conversation: PrivateChatConversation
    user_message: PrivateChatMessage
    assistant_message: PrivateChatMessage
    response_language: str

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not isinstance(self.conversation, PrivateChatConversation):
            raise ValueError(
                "conversation must be a PrivateChatConversation."
            )

        if not isinstance(self.user_message, PrivateChatMessage):
            raise ValueError(
                "user_message must be a PrivateChatMessage."
            )

        if not isinstance(self.assistant_message, PrivateChatMessage):
            raise ValueError(
                "assistant_message must be a PrivateChatMessage."
            )

        if (
            not isinstance(self.response_language, str)
            or not self.response_language.strip()
        ):
            raise ValueError("response_language cannot be empty.")

"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center provider health models.

Defines the domain contracts used to monitor AI service
provider availability and operational health.

Architecture:

Provider Health Probe
        |
        v
ProviderHealthObservation
        |
        v
ProviderHealthService
        |
        v
ProviderIncidentService
        |
        v
Escalation Service
        |
        v
AI Operations Center


Responsibilities:

- represent provider health states;
- represent individual health observations;
- represent provider outage information;
- represent health transitions;
- provide immutable domain contracts.

Does NOT:

- execute providers;
- perform health probes;
- persist data;
- send emails;
- modify Runtime routing;
- create autonomous failover.

Human approval remains mandatory for operational changes.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from enum import Enum


class ProviderHealthStatus(
    str,
    Enum,
):
    """
    Current operational health state of an AI provider.
    """

    HEALTHY = "healthy"

    DEGRADED = "degraded"

    UNAVAILABLE = "unavailable"

    UNKNOWN = "unknown"


class ProviderHealthTransition(
    str,
    Enum,
):
    """
    Significant transition in provider health.
    """

    RECOVERED = "recovered"

    DEGRADED = "degraded"

    UNAVAILABLE = "unavailable"

    UNKNOWN = "unknown"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ProviderHealthObservation:
    """
    Immutable observation produced by a provider health
    probe or equivalent Runtime health signal.

    Represents one point-in-time observation.

    Additional intelligence fields are optional to preserve
    backward compatibility with existing probes and tests.
    """

    provider: str

    model: str | None

    capability: str | None

    status: ProviderHealthStatus

    observed_at: datetime

    latency_ms: int | None = None

    error_type: str | None = None

    error_message: str | None = None

    consecutive_failures: int = 0

    consecutive_successes: int = 0

    #
    # Provider Intelligence fields
    #

    last_success_at: datetime | None = None

    last_failure_at: datetime | None = None

    average_latency_ms: float | None = None

    availability_percentage: float | None = None

    total_checks: int = 0

    successful_checks: int = 0

    failed_checks: int = 0


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ProviderHealthState:
    """
    Current calculated health state of a provider.

    Represents operational state derived from one or more
    health observations.

    It does not represent historical incident storage.
    """

    provider: str

    status: ProviderHealthStatus

    checked_at: datetime

    consecutive_failures: int

    consecutive_successes: int

    last_success_at: datetime | None = None

    last_failure_at: datetime | None = None

    degraded_since: datetime | None = None

    unavailable_since: datetime | None = None

    average_latency_ms: float | None = None

    availability_percentage: float | None = None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ProviderIncident:
    """
    Provider availability incident.

    Represents a sustained operational problem,
    not an individual failed request.
    """

    incident_id: str

    provider: str

    status: ProviderHealthStatus

    started_at: datetime

    last_observed_at: datetime

    recovered_at: datetime | None = None

    affected_capabilities: tuple[
        str,
        ...,
    ] = ()

    consecutive_failures: int = 0

    resolution_message: str | None = None


    @property
    def is_open(self) -> bool:
        """
        Return whether the provider incident remains open.
        """

        return self.recovered_at is None


    @property
    def downtime_seconds(self) -> float | None:
        """
        Return completed downtime in seconds.
        """

        if self.recovered_at is None:
            return None

        return (
            self.recovered_at
            -
            self.started_at
        ).total_seconds()


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ProviderHealthTransitionEvent:
    """
    Significant provider health transition.

    Used by application services to determine whether an
    incident should be created, updated, recovered, or
    escalated.
    """

    provider: str

    transition: ProviderHealthTransition

    previous_status: ProviderHealthStatus

    current_status: ProviderHealthStatus

    occurred_at: datetime

    consecutive_failures: int

    consecutive_successes: int

    affected_capabilities: tuple[
        str,
        ...,
    ] = ()
----- C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\provider_management\provider_health_read_model.py -----
"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center provider health read model.

Provides a presentation-independent operational read model
for provider health information.

Architecture:

Provider Health Observation
        |
        v
ProviderHealthService
        |
        v
Provider Health Read Model
        |
        +-------------------+
        |                   |
        v                   v
Operations Center       Future Agent
Dashboard              Operations
        |                   |
        +---------+---------+
                  |
                  v
          Operational Intelligence


Responsibilities:

- expose provider health information in a dashboard-safe
  read model;
- retain provider/model/capability attribution;
- expose current health state;
- expose failure and recovery timing information;
- remain read-only.

Does NOT:

- execute providers;
- perform health probes;
- change Runtime routing;
- create incidents;
- send emails;
- approve recommendations;
- perform autonomous failover.

This module is an operational read model, not a second
health-management engine.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_models import (
    ProviderHealthObservation,
    ProviderHealthState,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ProviderHealthReadModel:
    """
    Dashboard-safe provider health representation.

    Model and capability attribution are retained from the
    latest provider health observation while operational
    health state comes from ProviderHealthService.
    """

    provider: str

    model: str | None

    capability: str | None

    status: str

    checked_at: datetime

    consecutive_failures: int

    consecutive_successes: int

    last_success_at: datetime | None

    last_failure_at: datetime | None

    degraded_since: datetime | None

    unavailable_since: datetime | None

    active: bool = True


class ProviderHealthReadModelService:
    """
    Builds read-only provider health projections.

    This service does not calculate provider health itself.
    Health state remains owned by ProviderHealthService.
    """

    def build(
        self,
        *,
        observation: ProviderHealthObservation,
        state: ProviderHealthState,
    ) -> ProviderHealthReadModel:
        """
        Build a provider health read model from the latest
        observation and calculated health state.
        """

        if observation is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider health observation cannot be None."
            )

        if state is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider health state cannot be None."
            )

        if (
            observation.provider
            != state.provider
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Observation provider and health state "
                "provider must match."
            )

        return ProviderHealthReadModel(
            provider=state.provider,
            model=observation.model,
            capability=observation.capability,
            status=state.status.value,
            checked_at=state.checked_at,
            consecutive_failures=(
                state.consecutive_failures
            ),
            consecutive_successes=(
                state.consecutive_successes
            ),
            last_success_at=(
                state.last_success_at
            ),
            last_failure_at=(
                state.last_failure_at
            ),
            degraded_since=(
                state.degraded_since
            ),
            unavailable_since=(
                state.unavailable_since
            ),
            active=True,
        )

==================== SQLALCHEMY REPOSITORY EXAMPLES ====================
----- C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\infrastructure\announcements\sqlalchemy_publication_execution_repository.py -----
"""SQLAlchemy durable execution repository for WS-22.

Uses the canonical EasyChatBot SQLAlchemy Base. This module deliberately does
not create a second DeclarativeBase/metadata registry.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from uuid import UUID

from sqlalchemy import DateTime, Integer, String, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession, async_sessionmaker
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column

from app.application.announcements.execution.durable_contracts import (
    DurableExecutionStatus,
    PublicationExecutionRecord,
    PublicationExecutionRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.database import Base


class PublicationExecutionRecordModel(Base):
    """Durable operational state for a WS-22 publication execution."""

    __tablename__ = "announcement_publication_executions"

    publication_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(36), nullable=False)
    idempotency_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(255), primary_key=True
    )
    correlation_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(255),
        nullable=False,
    )
    attempt: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer,
        nullable=False,
        default=1,
    )
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(32),
        nullable=False,
    )
    provider_message_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(255),
        nullable=True,
    )
    error_code: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(100),
        nullable=True,
    )
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True),
        nullable=False,
    )
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True),
        nullable=False,
    )


class SqlAlchemyPublicationExecutionRepository(
    PublicationExecutionRepository
):
    """Durable repository using the existing injected async session factory."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        session_factory: async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession],
    ) -> None:
        if session_factory is None:
            raise ValueError("session_factory cannot be None.")

        self._session_factory = session_factory

    async def get_by_idempotency_key(
        self,
        idempotency_key: str,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionRecord | None:
        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            result = await session.execute(
                select(PublicationExecutionRecordModel).where(
                    PublicationExecutionRecordModel.idempotency_key
                    == idempotency_key
                )
            )
            model = result.scalar_one_or_none()

            return self._to_domain(model) if model else None

    async def create_started(
        self,
        *,
        publication_id,
        idempotency_key,
        correlation_id,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionRecord:
        """Create the first durable execution attempt."""

        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

        model = PublicationExecutionRecordModel(
            publication_id=str(publication_id),
            idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
            correlation_id=correlation_id,
            attempt=1,
            status=DurableExecutionStatus.STARTED.value,
            created_at=now,
            updated_at=now,
        )

        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            session.add(model)
            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(model)

            return self._to_domain(model)

    async def mark_succeeded(
        self,
        *,
        idempotency_key,
        provider_message_id,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionRecord:
        """Persist successful completion without changing the attempt count."""

        return await self._update(
            idempotency_key,
            status=DurableExecutionStatus.SUCCEEDED.value,
            provider_message_id=provider_message_id,
            error_code=None,
        )

    async def mark_failed(
        self,
        *,
        idempotency_key,
        error_code,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionRecord:
        """Persist failure while retaining the current attempt number."""

        return await self._update(
            idempotency_key,
            status=DurableExecutionStatus.FAILED.value,
            error_code=error_code,
        )

    async def start_retry(
        self,
        *,
        idempotency_key: str,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionRecord:
        """Increment the durable attempt counter for a retry.

        A retry is valid only for an existing FAILED execution. The same
        idempotency record is retained; no second database row is created.
        """

        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            result = await session.execute(
                select(PublicationExecutionRecordModel).where(
                    PublicationExecutionRecordModel.idempotency_key
                    == idempotency_key
                )
            )

            model = result.scalar_one()

            if model.status != DurableExecutionStatus.FAILED.value:
                raise ValueError(
                    "Only failed executions can be retried."
                )

            model.attempt += 1
            model.status = DurableExecutionStatus.STARTED.value
            model.error_code = None
            model.provider_message_id = None
            model.updated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(model)

            return self._to_domain(model)

    async def _update(
        self,
        idempotency_key,
        **values,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionRecord:
        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            result = await session.execute(
                select(PublicationExecutionRecordModel).where(
                    PublicationExecutionRecordModel.idempotency_key
                    == idempotency_key
                )
            )

            model = result.scalar_one()

            for key, value in values.items():
                setattr(model, key, value)

            model.updated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(model)

            return self._to_domain(model)

    @staticmethod
    def _to_domain(
        model: PublicationExecutionRecordModel,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionRecord:
        return PublicationExecutionRecord(
            publication_id=UUID(model.publication_id),
            idempotency_key=model.idempotency_key,
            correlation_id=model.correlation_id,
            attempt=model.attempt,
            status=DurableExecutionStatus(model.status),
            provider_message_id=model.provider_message_id,
            error_code=model.error_code,
            created_at=model.created_at,
            updated_at=model.updated_at,
        )
----- C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\infrastructure\announcements\sqlalchemy_publication_repository.py -----
"""PostgreSQL adapter for the existing PublicationRepository contract."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone

from sqlalchemy import DateTime, Integer, String, Text, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession, async_sessionmaker
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column

from app.application.announcements.repositories import PublicationRepository
from app.domain.announcements.publication import Publication
from app.domain.announcements.template_models import (
    PublicationStatus,
    PublicationType,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.database import Base


class PublicationModel(Base):
    """Canonical durable announcement publication state."""

    __tablename__ = "announcement_publications"

    publication_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(255), primary_key=True)
    template_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(255), nullable=False)
    template_version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False)
    publication_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)
    title: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120), nullable=False)
    content: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=False)
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False, index=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False)
    approved_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    published_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    failure_reason: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(Text)


class SqlAlchemyPublicationRepository(PublicationRepository):
    """Async PostgreSQL adapter for the existing repository contract."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        session_factory: async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession],
    ) -> None:
        if session_factory is None:
            raise ValueError("session_factory cannot be None.")
        self._session_factory = session_factory

    def _to_domain(self, model: PublicationModel) -> Publication:
        return Publication(
            publication_id=model.publication_id,
            template_id=model.template_id,
            template_version=model.template_version,
            publication_type=PublicationType(model.publication_type),
            title=model.title,
            content=model.content,
            status=PublicationStatus(model.status),
            created_at=model.created_at,
            approved_at=model.approved_at,
            published_at=model.published_at,
            failure_reason=model.failure_reason,
        )

    def _from_domain(self, publication: Publication) -> PublicationModel:
        return PublicationModel(
            publication_id=publication.publication_id,
            template_id=publication.template_id,
            template_version=publication.template_version,
            publication_type=publication.publication_type.value,
            title=publication.title,
            content=publication.content,
            status=publication.status.value,
            created_at=publication.created_at or datetime.now(timezone.utc),
            approved_at=publication.approved_at,
            published_at=publication.published_at,
            failure_reason=publication.failure_reason,
        )

    async def save_async(self, publication: Publication) -> None:
        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            model = await session.get(
                PublicationModel,
                publication.publication_id,
            )
            if model is None:
                session.add(self._from_domain(publication))
            else:
                model.template_id = publication.template_id
                model.template_version = publication.template_version
                model.publication_type = publication.publication_type.value
                model.title = publication.title
                model.content = publication.content
                model.status = publication.status.value
                model.created_at = publication.created_at or model.created_at
                model.approved_at = publication.approved_at
                model.published_at = publication.published_at
                model.failure_reason = publication.failure_reason
            await session.commit()

    async def get_async(self, publication_id: str) -> Publication | None:
        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            model = await session.get(PublicationModel, publication_id)
            return self._to_domain(model) if model else None

    async def list_all_async(self) -> tuple[Publication, ...]:
        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            result = await session.execute(
                select(PublicationModel).order_by(PublicationModel.created_at)
            )
            return tuple(self._to_domain(model) for model in result.scalars().all())

    # The existing repository contract is synchronous. These methods make
    # accidental async DB use explicit rather than silently blocking.
    def save(self, publication: Publication) -> None:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "SqlAlchemyPublicationRepository requires its async boundary; "
            "use save_async()."
        )

    def get(self, publication_id: str) -> Publication | None:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "SqlAlchemyPublicationRepository requires its async boundary; "
            "use get_async()."
        )

    def list_all(self) -> tuple[Publication, ...]:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "SqlAlchemyPublicationRepository requires its async boundary; "
            "use list_all_async()."
        )
