"""PostgreSQL read/append adapter for PI delivery audit history."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping
from typing import Any
from uuid import UUID

from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession, async_sessionmaker

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery_audit import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRecord,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRepository,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus,
)


class SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRepository(
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRepository
):
    _TABLE = "platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery_audit"

    def __init__(self, session_factory: async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession]) -> None:
        if session_factory is None:
            raise ValueError("session_factory cannot be None.")
        self._session_factory = session_factory

    async def append(self, record: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRecord) -> None:
        if record.occurred_at is None:
            raise ValueError("occurred_at is required for durable audit persistence.")
        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            await self._insert(session, record)
            await session.commit()

    async def list_by_notification_key(self, notification_key: str):
        if not notification_key.strip():
            raise ValueError("notification_key must be nonblank.")
        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            result = await session.execute(
                text(
                    f"""
                    SELECT audit_id, notification_key, recommendation_id,
                           risk_decision_id, review_item_id, correlation_id,
                           attempt, status, provider_message_id, error_code,
                           occurred_at
                    FROM {self._TABLE}
                    WHERE notification_key = :notification_key
                    ORDER BY occurred_at ASC, audit_id ASC
                    """
                ),
                {"notification_key": notification_key},
            )
            return tuple(self._to_record(row) for row in result.mappings().all())

    @classmethod
    async def insert_with_session(
        cls,
        session: AsyncSession,
        record: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRecord,
    ) -> None:
        if record.occurred_at is None:
            raise ValueError("occurred_at is required for durable audit persistence.")
        await cls._insert(session, record)

    @classmethod
    async def _insert(
        cls,
        session: AsyncSession,
        record: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRecord,
    ) -> None:
        await session.execute(
            text(
                f"""
                INSERT INTO {cls._TABLE} (
                    audit_id, notification_key, recommendation_id,
                    risk_decision_id, review_item_id, correlation_id,
                    attempt, status, provider_message_id, error_code,
                    occurred_at
                )
                VALUES (
                    :audit_id, :notification_key, :recommendation_id,
                    :risk_decision_id, :review_item_id, :correlation_id,
                    :attempt, :status, :provider_message_id, :error_code,
                    :occurred_at
                )
                """
            ),
            {
                "audit_id": record.audit_id,
                "notification_key": record.notification_key,
                "recommendation_id": record.recommendation_id,
                "risk_decision_id": record.risk_decision_id,
                "review_item_id": record.review_item_id,
                "correlation_id": record.correlation_id,
                "attempt": record.attempt,
                "status": record.status.value,
                "provider_message_id": record.provider_message_id,
                "error_code": record.error_code,
                "occurred_at": record.occurred_at,
            },
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _to_record(row: Mapping[str, Any]) -> PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRecord:
        return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRecord(
            audit_id=UUID(str(row["audit_id"])),
            notification_key=str(row["notification_key"]),
            recommendation_id=str(row["recommendation_id"]),
            risk_decision_id=str(row["risk_decision_id"]),
            review_item_id=str(row["review_item_id"]),
            correlation_id=str(row["correlation_id"]),
            attempt=int(row["attempt"]),
            status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus(str(row["status"])),
            provider_message_id=row["provider_message_id"],
            error_code=row["error_code"],
            occurred_at=row["occurred_at"],
        )
----- C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\infrastructure\operations_center\sqlalchemy_platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery_repository.py -----
"""PostgreSQL persistence adapter for PI escalation delivery."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any
from uuid import uuid4

from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession, async_sessionmaker

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRecord,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRepository,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery_audit import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRecord,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus,
)
from app.infrastructure.operations_center.sqlalchemy_platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery_audit_repository import (
    SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRepository,
)


class SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRepository(
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRepository
):
    _TABLE = "platform_intelligence_escalation_deliveries"

    def __init__(self, session_factory: async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession]) -> None:
        if session_factory is None:
            raise ValueError("session_factory cannot be None.")
        self._session_factory = session_factory

    async def get_by_notification_key(self, notification_key: str):
        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            result = await session.execute(
                text(
                    f"""
                    SELECT notification_key, recommendation_id,
                           risk_decision_id, review_item_id, correlation_id,
                           attempt, status, provider_message_id, error_code,
                           created_at, updated_at
                    FROM {self._TABLE}
                    WHERE notification_key = :notification_key
                    """
                ),
                {"notification_key": notification_key},
            )
            row = result.mappings().first()
            return None if row is None else self._to_record(row)

    async def list_retry_candidates(self, *, limit: int):
        if limit < 1:
            raise ValueError("limit must be at least 1.")
        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            result = await session.execute(
                text(
                    f"""
                    SELECT notification_key, recommendation_id,
                           risk_decision_id, review_item_id, correlation_id,
                           attempt, status, provider_message_id, error_code,
                           created_at, updated_at
                    FROM {self._TABLE}
                    WHERE status IN (:failed, :started)
                    ORDER BY updated_at ASC NULLS FIRST, notification_key ASC
                    LIMIT :limit
                    """
                ),
                {
                    "failed": PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED.value,
                    "started": PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED.value,
                    "limit": limit,
                },
            )
            return tuple(self._to_record(row) for row in result.mappings().all())

    async def claim_retry_candidate(
        self, *, notification_key: str, claim_token: str,
        claimed_at: datetime, lease_expires_at: datetime,
    ) -> bool:
        if not notification_key.strip():
            raise ValueError("notification_key must be nonblank.")
        if not claim_token.strip():
            raise ValueError("claim_token must be nonblank.")
        if claimed_at.tzinfo is None or claimed_at.utcoffset() is None:
            raise ValueError("claimed_at must be timezone-aware.")
        if lease_expires_at.tzinfo is None or lease_expires_at.utcoffset() is None:
            raise ValueError("lease_expires_at must be timezone-aware.")
        if lease_expires_at <= claimed_at:
            raise ValueError("lease_expires_at must be after claimed_at.")

        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            result = await session.execute(
                text(
                    f"""
                    UPDATE {self._TABLE}
                    SET claim_token = :claim_token,
                        claimed_at = :claimed_at,
                        lease_expires_at = :lease_expires_at
                    WHERE notification_key = :notification_key
                      AND status IN (:failed, :started)
                      AND (
                          lease_expires_at IS NULL
                          OR lease_expires_at <= :claimed_at
                      )
                    """
                ),
                {
                    "notification_key": notification_key,
                    "claim_token": claim_token,
                    "claimed_at": claimed_at,
                    "lease_expires_at": lease_expires_at,
                    "failed": PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED.value,
                    "started": PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED.value,
                },
            )
            await session.commit()
            return result.rowcount == 1

    async def create_started(
        self, *, notification_key: str, recommendation_id: str,
        risk_decision_id: str, review_item_id: str, correlation_id: str,
    ):
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            await session.execute(
                text(
                    f"""
                    INSERT INTO {self._TABLE} (
                        notification_key, recommendation_id, risk_decision_id,
                        review_item_id, correlation_id, attempt, status,
                        provider_message_id, error_code, created_at, updated_at,
                        claim_token, claimed_at, lease_expires_at
                    )
                    VALUES (
                        :notification_key, :recommendation_id, :risk_decision_id,
                        :review_item_id, :correlation_id, 1, :status,
                        NULL, NULL, :created_at, :updated_at,
                        NULL, NULL, NULL
                    )
                    """
                ),
                {
                    "notification_key": notification_key,
                    "recommendation_id": recommendation_id,
                    "risk_decision_id": risk_decision_id,
                    "review_item_id": review_item_id,
                    "correlation_id": correlation_id,
                    "status": PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED.value,
                    "created_at": now,
                    "updated_at": now,
                },
            )
            await self._insert_audit(
                session,
                notification_key=notification_key,
                recommendation_id=recommendation_id,
                risk_decision_id=risk_decision_id,
                review_item_id=review_item_id,
                correlation_id=correlation_id,
                attempt=1,
                status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus.STARTED,
                occurred_at=now,
            )
            await session.commit()

        record = await self.get_by_notification_key(notification_key)
        if record is None:
            raise RuntimeError("Created PI escalation delivery could not be reloaded.")
        return record

    async def mark_succeeded(
        self, *, notification_key: str, provider_message_id: str | None,
    ):
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            result = await session.execute(
                text(
                    f"""
                    UPDATE {self._TABLE}
                    SET status = :status,
                        provider_message_id = :provider_message_id,
                        error_code = NULL,
                        updated_at = :updated_at,
                        claim_token = NULL,
                        claimed_at = NULL,
                        lease_expires_at = NULL
                    WHERE notification_key = :notification_key
                    RETURNING notification_key, recommendation_id,
                              risk_decision_id, review_item_id, correlation_id,
                              attempt, status, provider_message_id,
                              error_code, created_at, updated_at
                    """
                ),
                {
                    "notification_key": notification_key,
                    "status": PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.SUCCEEDED.value,
                    "provider_message_id": provider_message_id,
                    "updated_at": now,
                },
            )
            row = result.mappings().first()
            if row is None:
                await session.rollback()
                raise KeyError(f"Unknown PI escalation notification key: {notification_key}")

            record = self._to_record(row)
            if not provider_message_id:
                await session.rollback()
                raise ValueError("Successful PI delivery requires provider_message_id.")

            await self._insert_audit(
                session,
                notification_key=record.notification_key,
                recommendation_id=record.recommendation_id,
                risk_decision_id=record.risk_decision_id,
                review_item_id=record.review_item_id,
                correlation_id=record.correlation_id,
                attempt=record.attempt,
                status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus.SUCCEEDED,
                provider_message_id=provider_message_id,
                occurred_at=now,
            )
            await session.commit()
            return record

    async def mark_failed(self, *, notification_key: str, error_code: str):
        if not error_code.strip():
            raise ValueError("error_code must be nonblank.")
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            result = await session.execute(
                text(
                    f"""
                    UPDATE {self._TABLE}
                    SET status = :status,
                        provider_message_id = NULL,
                        error_code = :error_code,
                        updated_at = :updated_at,
                        claim_token = NULL,
                        claimed_at = NULL,
                        lease_expires_at = NULL
                    WHERE notification_key = :notification_key
                    RETURNING notification_key, recommendation_id,
                              risk_decision_id, review_item_id, correlation_id,
                              attempt, status, provider_message_id,
                              error_code, created_at, updated_at
                    """
                ),
                {
                    "notification_key": notification_key,
                    "status": PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED.value,
                    "error_code": error_code,
                    "updated_at": now,
                },
            )
            row = result.mappings().first()
            if row is None:
                await session.rollback()
                raise KeyError(f"Unknown PI escalation notification key: {notification_key}")

            record = self._to_record(row)
            await self._insert_audit(
                session,
                notification_key=record.notification_key,
                recommendation_id=record.recommendation_id,
                risk_decision_id=record.risk_decision_id,
                review_item_id=record.review_item_id,
                correlation_id=record.correlation_id,
                attempt=record.attempt,
                status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus.FAILED,
                error_code=error_code,
                occurred_at=now,
            )
            await session.commit()
            return record

    async def start_retry(self, *, notification_key: str):
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            result = await session.execute(
                text(
                    f"""
                    UPDATE {self._TABLE}
                    SET attempt = attempt + 1,
                        status = :started,
                        provider_message_id = NULL,
                        error_code = NULL,
                        updated_at = :updated_at,
                        claim_token = NULL,
                        claimed_at = NULL,
                        lease_expires_at = NULL
                    WHERE notification_key = :notification_key
                      AND status = :failed
                    RETURNING notification_key, recommendation_id,
                              risk_decision_id, review_item_id, correlation_id,
                              attempt, status, provider_message_id,
                              error_code, created_at, updated_at
                    """
                ),
                {
                    "notification_key": notification_key,
                    "started": PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED.value,
                    "failed": PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED.value,
                    "updated_at": now,
                },
            )
            row = result.mappings().first()
            if row is None:
                await session.rollback()
                raise RuntimeError(
                    "PI escalation delivery can only be retried from FAILED."
                )

            record = self._to_record(row)
            await self._insert_audit(
                session,
                notification_key=record.notification_key,
                recommendation_id=record.recommendation_id,
                risk_decision_id=record.risk_decision_id,
                review_item_id=record.review_item_id,
                correlation_id=record.correlation_id,
                attempt=record.attempt,
                status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus.STARTED,
                occurred_at=now,
            )
            await session.commit()
            return record

    @staticmethod
    async def _insert_audit(
        session: AsyncSession,
        *,
        notification_key: str,
        recommendation_id: str,
        risk_decision_id: str,
        review_item_id: str,
        correlation_id: str,
        attempt: int,
        status: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus,
        occurred_at: datetime,
        provider_message_id: str | None = None,
        error_code: str | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        record = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRecord(
            audit_id=uuid4(),
            notification_key=notification_key,
            recommendation_id=recommendation_id,
            risk_decision_id=risk_decision_id,
            review_item_id=review_item_id,
            correlation_id=correlation_id,
            attempt=attempt,
            status=status,
            provider_message_id=provider_message_id,
            error_code=error_code,
            occurred_at=occurred_at,
        )
        await SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRepository.insert_with_session(
            session, record
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _to_record(row: Mapping[str, Any]):
        return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRecord(
            notification_key=str(row["notification_key"]),
            recommendation_id=str(row["recommendation_id"]),
            risk_decision_id=str(row["risk_decision_id"]),
            review_item_id=str(row["review_item_id"]),
            correlation_id=str(row["correlation_id"]),
            attempt=int(row["attempt"]),
            status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus(str(row["status"])),
            provider_message_id=row["provider_message_id"],
            error_code=row["error_code"],
            created_at=row["created_at"],
            updated_at=row["updated_at"],
        )

def make_project_control() -> ProjectControlService:
    registry = InMemoryProjectRegistry()

    registry.save(
        ProjectDefinition(
            project_id="easychatbot",
            name="EasyChatBot",
        )
    )

    return ProjectControlService(
        registry=registry,
        sources=(AuthorizationTestSource(),),
        snapshots=InMemorySnapshotRepository(),
    )


def make_router():
    return create_project_control_router(
        make_project_control(),
    )


def make_app() -> FastAPI:
    application = FastAPI()

    application.include_router(
        make_router(),
    )

    return application


def make_principal(
    *,
    roles: frozenset[str],
) -> Principal:
    return Principal(
        user_id="authorization-test-user",
        email="operator@example.test",
        email_verified=True,
        account_status=AccountStatus.ACTIVE,
        roles=roles,
    )


def test_beta_user_does_not_have_functionality_control_permission():
    principal = make_principal(
        roles=frozenset({"beta_user"}),
    )

    dependency = require_permission(
        Permission.FUNCTIONALITY_CONTROL,
    )

    application = FastAPI()

    @application.get("/test")
    async def protected(
        _: Principal = Depends(dependency),
    ):
        return {"ok": True}

    # Override the identity dependency, not the permission dependency.
    #
    # This preserves the real authorization path:
    #
    # get_current_principal
    #       -> require_permission(...)
    #       -> AuthorizationService
    #       -> PermissionDeniedError
    #       -> HTTP 403
    application.dependency_overrides[
        get_current_principal
    ] = lambda: principal

    with TestClient(application) as client:
        response = client.get("/test")

    assert response.status_code == 403


def test_operator_has_functionality_control_permission():
    principal = make_principal(
        roles=frozenset({"operator"}),
    )

    dependency = require_permission(
        Permission.FUNCTIONALITY_CONTROL,
    )

    application = FastAPI()

    @application.get("/test")
    async def protected(
        _: Principal = Depends(dependency),
    ):
        return {"ok": True}

    # Override only identity so that the real authorization
    # service evaluates the operator's permission.
    application.dependency_overrides[
        get_current_principal
    ] = lambda: principal

    with TestClient(application) as client:
        response = client.get("/test")

    assert response.status_code == 200


def test_project_control_router_uses_functionality_control_permission():
    router = make_router()

    routes = [
        route
        for route in router.routes
        if hasattr(route, "path")
        and route.path
        == "/project-control/projects/{project_id}"
    ]

    assert len(routes) == 1

    route = routes[0]

    assert route.methods == {"GET"}

    dependency_calls = {
        dependency.call
        for dependency in route.dependant.dependencies
    }

    assert len(dependency_calls) == 1

    permission_dependency = next(
        iter(dependency_calls)
    )

    assert permission_dependency is not None

from __future__ import annotations

from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch

import pytest

from app.infrastructure.telegram.telegram_announcement_client import (
    TelegramAnnouncementClient,
)


pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_academy_activation_through_fastapi_lifespan():
    """
    Controlled FastAPI lifespan activation smoke test.

    Verifies the complete application lifecycle:

        FastAPI lifespan
            â†“
        existing shared scheduler
            â†“
        Academy registration
            â†“
        scheduler running
            â†“
        clean shutdown

    No real email or Telegram delivery is performed.
    """

    fake_engine = MagicMock(
        name="auth_engine",
    )

    fake_session_factory = MagicMock(
        name="auth_session_factory",
    )

    fake_telegram_client = MagicMock(
        spec=TelegramAnnouncementClient,
        name="announcement_telegram_client",
    )

    fake_announcement_publisher = MagicMock(
        name="announcement_publisher",
    )

    academy_workflow = MagicMock(
        name="academy_one_week_workflow",
    )

    academy_workflow.run = MagicMock(
        name="academy_workflow_run",
    )

    scheduler_runtime = MagicMock(
        name="scheduler_runtime",
    )

    scheduler_runtime.start = MagicMock(
        name="scheduler_runtime_start",
    )

    scheduler_runtime.stop = MagicMock(
        name="scheduler_runtime_stop",
    )

    with (
        patch.dict(
            "os.environ",
            {
                "EASYCHATBOT_ACADEMY_SCHEDULER_ENABLED": "true",
                "EASYCHATBOT_ACADEMY_FIRST_RUN_ENABLED": "true",
            },
            clear=False,
        ),
        patch(
            "app.main.create_async_database_engine",
            return_value=fake_engine,
        ),
        patch(
            "app.main.create_async_session_factory",
            return_value=fake_session_factory,
        ),
        patch(
            "app.main.PostgreSQLAuthenticationRepository",
            return_value=MagicMock(
                name="auth_repository",
            ),
        ),
        patch(
            "app.main._create_announcement_components",
            return_value=(
                fake_telegram_client,
                fake_announcement_publisher,
            ),
        ),
        patch(
            "app.main.create_academy_one_week_workflow",
            return_value=academy_workflow,
        ),
        patch(
            "app.main.create_cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime",
            return_value=scheduler_runtime,
        ) as scheduler_factory,
    ):
        from app.main import create_application

        application = create_application()

        assert (
            application.state.scheduler_runtime
            is scheduler_runtime
        )

        scheduler_factory.assert_called_once_with(
            academy_one_week_workflow=academy_workflow,
        )

        async with application.router.lifespan_context(
            application,
        ):
            scheduler_runtime.start.assert_called_once()

            scheduler_runtime.stop.assert_not_called()

            academy_workflow.run.assert_not_called()

            fake_telegram_client.method_calls == []

        scheduler_runtime.stop.assert_called_once()

    fake_telegram_client.method_calls == []

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, status
from httpx import ASGITransport, AsyncClient

from app.api.announcement_operations_approval import (
    announcement_publish_permission,
    create_announcement_operations_approval_router,
)
from app.application.announcements.approval.async_approval_service import (
    AsyncAnnouncementPublicationApprovalService,
)
from app.domain.announcements.publication import Publication
from app.domain.announcements.template_models import (
    PublicationStatus,
    PublicationType,
)


class FakeAsyncRepository:
    def __init__(self, publication: Publication | None) -> None:
        self.publication = publication
        self.saved: list[Publication] = []

    async def get_async(self, publication_id: str) -> Publication | None:
        if (
            self.publication is not None
            and self.publication.publication_id == publication_id
        ):
            return self.publication
        return None

    async def save_async(self, publication: Publication) -> None:
        self.saved.append(publication)
        self.publication = publication


class AuditRecorder:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.events: list[dict] = []

    async def record_approval(self, **kwargs) -> None:
        self.events.append(kwargs)


def make_publication(status_value: PublicationStatus) -> Publication:
    return Publication(
        publication_id="ws23-1-3-api-test",
        template_id="beta-update",
        template_version=1,
        publication_type=PublicationType.BETA_UPDATE,
        title="Beta update",
        content="Test announcement",
        status=status_value,
        created_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
    )


def make_principal(user_id: str = "operator-test") -> SimpleNamespace:
    return SimpleNamespace(user_id=user_id)


async def deny_announcement_publish_permission() -> None:
    raise HTTPException(
        status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
        detail="Permission denied.",
    )


async def allow_announcement_publish_permission() -> SimpleNamespace:
    return make_principal()


def make_service(
    repository: FakeAsyncRepository,
    audit: AuditRecorder | None = None,
) -> AsyncAnnouncementPublicationApprovalService:
    return AsyncAnnouncementPublicationApprovalService(
        publications=repository,
        audit=audit or AuditRecorder(),
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_approval_api_requires_announcement_publish_permission() -> None:
    repository = FakeAsyncRepository(
        make_publication(PublicationStatus.PENDING_APPROVAL)
    )

    service = make_service(repository)

    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(
        create_announcement_operations_approval_router(
            approval_service=service,
        )
    )

    app.dependency_overrides[announcement_publish_permission] = (
        deny_announcement_publish_permission
    )

    async with AsyncClient(
        transport=ASGITransport(app=app),
        base_url="http://test",
    ) as client:
        response = await client.post(
            "/api/announcement-operations/publications/"
            "ws23-1-3-api-test/approve",
            json={},
        )

    assert response.status_code == 403
    assert (
        repository.publication is not None
        and repository.publication.status
        is PublicationStatus.PENDING_APPROVAL
    )
    assert repository.saved == []


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_approval_api_approves_pending_publication() -> None:
    repository = FakeAsyncRepository(
        make_publication(PublicationStatus.PENDING_APPROVAL)
    )
    audit = AuditRecorder()

    service = make_service(repository, audit)

    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(
        create_announcement_operations_approval_router(
            approval_service=service,
        )
    )

    app.dependency_overrides[announcement_publish_permission] = (
        allow_announcement_publish_permission
    )

    async with AsyncClient(
        transport=ASGITransport(app=app),
        base_url="http://test",
    ) as client:
        response = await client.post(
            "/api/announcement-operations/publications/"
            "ws23-1-3-api-test/approve",
            json={},
        )

    assert response.status_code == 200
    assert response.json()["publication_id"] == "ws23-1-3-api-test"
    assert response.json()["status"] == "approved"

    assert repository.publication is not None
    assert repository.publication.status is PublicationStatus.APPROVED

    assert len(repository.saved) == 1
    assert len(audit.events) == 1


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_approval_api_ignores_caller_supplied_actor() -> None:
    repository = FakeAsyncRepository(
        make_publication(PublicationStatus.PENDING_APPROVAL)
    )
    audit = AuditRecorder()

    service = make_service(repository, audit)

    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(
        create_announcement_operations_approval_router(
            approval_service=service,
        )
    )

    app.dependency_overrides[announcement_publish_permission] = (
        allow_announcement_publish_permission
    )

    async with AsyncClient(
        transport=ASGITransport(app=app),
        base_url="http://test",
    ) as client:
        response = await client.post(
            "/api/announcement-operations/publications/"
            "ws23-1-3-api-test/approve",
            json={"actor": "attacker-controlled-actor"},
        )

    assert response.status_code == 200

    assert repository.publication is not None
    assert repository.publication.status is PublicationStatus.APPROVED

    assert len(audit.events) == 1
    assert audit.events[0]["actor"] == "operator-test"
    assert audit.events[0]["actor"] != "attacker-controlled-actor"

        response = await client.post(
            "/api/announcement-operations/publications/"

            json={},
        )

    assert response.status_code == 409

    assert repository.publication is not None
    assert repository.publication.status is PublicationStatus.DRAFT

    assert repository.saved == []

from types import SimpleNamespace

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from fastapi import FastAPI

from app.api.operations_center import create_operations_center_router
from app.application.operations_center.dashboard_composition import (
    create_operations_center_composition,
)


class Source:
    def _item(self):
        return SimpleNamespace(
            review_item_id="R1",
            risk_decision_id="D1",
            risk_assessment_id="A1",
            scope="platform",
            risk_level="HIGH",
            risk_score=0.8,
            priority="HIGH",
            review_status="PENDING",
            decision_status="PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW",
            quality_gate_status="PASSED",
            evidence_references=("E1",),
            historical_references=("H1",),
            policy_id="P",
            policy_version="1",
            created_at=SimpleNamespace(
                isoformat=lambda: "2026-09-22T00:00:00+00:00"
            ),
            assigned_reviewer=None,
            assigned_at=None,
            review_due_at=None,
            rationale="test",
            recommendation_status="RECOMMENDED",
            recommendation_route="HUMAN_REVIEW",
            recommendation_reasons=("risk",),
            recommendation_id="REC1",
        )

    def list_reviews(self):
        return [self._item()]

    def get_review(self, review_item_id):
        return self._item() if review_item_id == "R1" else None

    def list_escalations(self):
        return [self._item()]


def _client(source=None, monkeypatch=None):
    app = FastAPI()
    if monkeypatch is not None:
        import app.api.operations_center as operations_center_module
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            operations_center_module,
            "require_permission",
            lambda _permission: (lambda: None),
        )
    composition = create_operations_center_composition(
        platform_intelligence_review_source=source
    )
    app.include_router(create_operations_center_router(composition=composition))
    return TestClient(app)


def test_existing_operations_center_dashboard_route_is_preserved(monkeypatch):
    client = _client(Source(), monkeypatch)
    response = client.get("/api/operations-center/dashboard")
    assert response.status_code == 200


def test_pi_reviews_are_exposed_through_existing_operations_center_router(monkeypatch):
    client = _client(Source(), monkeypatch)
    response = client.get("/api/operations-center/platform-intelligence/reviews")
    assert response.status_code == 200
    assert response.json()["count"] == 1


def test_pi_escalations_are_exposed_through_existing_operations_center_router(monkeypatch):
    client = _client(Source(), monkeypatch)
    response = client.get("/api/operations-center/platform-intelligence/escalations")
    assert response.status_code == 200
    assert response.json()["count"] == 1


def test_pi_review_detail_not_found_is_404(monkeypatch):
    client = _client(Source(), monkeypatch)
    response = client.get(
        "/api/operations-center/platform-intelligence/reviews/UNKNOWN"
    )
    assert response.status_code == 404


def test_pi_integration_fails_closed_when_source_is_not_configured(monkeypatch):
    client = _client(monkeypatch=monkeypatch)
    response = client.get("/api/operations-center/platform-intelligence/reviews")
    assert response.status_code == 503

        create_platform_intelligence_review_router(api=None)
    except ValueError as exc:
        assert str(exc) == "api is required."
    else:

        source=UnconfiguredPlatformIntelligenceReviewSource()

"""
Voice API Contract Tests.

Validates stable frontend contracts.

Covers:
- Trigger response contract.
- Notification acceptance contract.
- Reconnect contract.

Does NOT:
- Test domain logic.
- Test repositories.
- Test providers.
"""

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from app.main import app


def test_voice_trigger_contract() -> None:
    with TestClient(app) as client:
        response = client.post(
            "/voice/trigger",
            json={
                "learner_id": "learner-001",
                "character_id": "yeti",
                "world_id": "french_world",
                "language_code": "fr",
                "channel": "inbox",
            },
        )

    assert response.status_code == 200

    body = response.json()

    assert "voice_call_id" in body
    assert "invitation_id" in body
    assert "notification_id" in body


def test_voice_acceptance_contract() -> None:
    with TestClient(app) as client:
        trigger = client.post(
            "/voice/trigger",
            json={
                "learner_id": "learner-002",
                "character_id": "alix",
                "world_id": "french_world",
                "language_code": "fr",
                "channel": "inbox",
            },
        )

        assert trigger.status_code == 200

        notification_id = trigger.json()["notification_id"]

        response = client.post(
            f"/voice/notification/{notification_id}/accept"
        )

    assert response.status_code == 200

    body = response.json()

    assert "voice_call_id" in body
    assert "learner_id" in body
    assert "character_id" in body
    assert "session_id" in body
    assert "state" in body


def test_voice_reconnect_contract() -> None:
    with TestClient(app) as client:
        trigger = client.post(
            "/voice/trigger",
            json={
                "learner_id": "learner-003",
                "character_id": "yeti",
                "world_id": "french_world",
                "language_code": "fr",
                "channel": "inbox",
            },
        )

        assert trigger.status_code == 200

        voice_call_id = trigger.json()["voice_call_id"]

        response = client.post(
            f"/voice/call/{voice_call_id}/reconnect"
        )

    assert response.status_code == 200

    body = response.json()

    assert body["voice_call_id"] == voice_call_id
    assert "session_id" in body
    assert "state" in body


def test_unknown_voice_notification_contract() -> None:
    with TestClient(app) as client:
        response = client.post(
            "/voice/notification/unknown/accept"
        )

    assert response.status_code == 404


def test_unknown_voice_call_reconnect_contract() -> None:
    with TestClient(app) as client:
        response = client.post(
            "/voice/call/unknown/reconnect"
        )

    assert response.status_code == 404

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_review_api import (
    PlatformIntelligenceReviewAPI,
    PlatformIntelligenceReviewNotConfigured,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_review_source import (
    PlatformIntelligenceReviewSource,
)


class FakeSource(PlatformIntelligenceReviewSource):
    def __init__(self, items):
        self.items = tuple(items)

    def list_reviews(self):
        return self.items

    def get_review(self, review_item_id):
        return next(
            (item for item in self.items if item.review_item_id == review_item_id),
            None,
        )

    def list_escalations(self):
        return tuple(
            item
            for item in self.items
            if getattr(
                getattr(item, "recommendation_status", None),
                "value",
                getattr(item, "recommendation_status", None),
            )
            == "RECOMMENDED"
        )


def projection(*, recommended=False):
    return SimpleNamespace(
        review_item_id="review-1",
        risk_decision_id="decision-1",
        risk_assessment_id="assessment-1",
        scope="platform",
        risk_level=SimpleNamespace(value="HIGH"),
        risk_score=0.8,
        priority=SimpleNamespace(value="HIGH"),
        review_status=SimpleNamespace(value="IN_REVIEW"),
        decision_status=SimpleNamespace(value="PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW"),
        quality_gate_status=SimpleNamespace(value="PASSED"),
        evidence_references=("e1",),
        historical_references=("h1",),
        policy_id="risk-policy",
        policy_version="1.0",
        created_at=datetime(2026, 9, 22, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
        assigned_reviewer="reviewer-1",
        assigned_at=datetime(2026, 9, 22, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
        review_due_at=None,
        rationale="Governed review required.",
        recommendation_status=(
            SimpleNamespace(value="RECOMMENDED") if recommended else None
        ),
        recommendation_route=(
            SimpleNamespace(value="RISK_OWNER") if recommended else None
        ),
        recommendation_reasons=("risk_level=HIGH",) if recommended else (),
        recommendation_id="esc-1" if recommended else None,
        metadata={"sensitive": "must-not-leak"},
    )


def test_list_serializes_only_approved_projection_fields():
    api = PlatformIntelligenceReviewAPI(source=FakeSource([projection()]))

    result = api.list_reviews()

    assert result["status"] == "ready"
    assert result["count"] == 1
    assert result["items"][0]["risk_decision_id"] == "decision-1"
    assert "metadata" not in result["items"][0]


def test_get_missing_returns_none():
    api = PlatformIntelligenceReviewAPI(source=FakeSource([]))

    assert api.get_review("missing") is None


def test_escalations_only_include_recommended_items():
    api = PlatformIntelligenceReviewAPI(
        source=FakeSource([projection(), projection(recommended=True)])
    )

    result = api.list_escalations()

    assert result["count"] == 1
    assert result["items"][0]["recommendation_status"] == "RECOMMENDED"


def test_unconfigured_source_is_explicitly_not_configured():
    api = PlatformIntelligenceReviewAPI(source=None)

    with pytest.raises(PlatformIntelligenceReviewNotConfigured):
        api.list_reviews()

from types import SimpleNamespace

from app.application.operations_center.dashboard_composition import (
    create_operations_center_composition,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_review_api import (
    PlatformIntelligenceReviewAPI,
)


class Source:
    def list_reviews(self):
        return [
            SimpleNamespace(
                review_item_id="R1",
                risk_decision_id="D1",
                risk_assessment_id="A1",
                scope="platform",
                risk_level="HIGH",
                risk_score=0.8,
                priority="HIGH",
                review_status="PENDING",
                decision_status="PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW",
                quality_gate_status="PASSED",
                evidence_references=("E1",),
                historical_references=("H1",),
                policy_id="P",
                policy_version="1",
                created_at=SimpleNamespace(
                    isoformat=lambda: "2026-09-22T00:00:00+00:00"
                ),
                assigned_reviewer=None,
                assigned_at=None,
                review_due_at=None,
                rationale="test",
                recommendation_status="RECOMMENDED",
                recommendation_route="HUMAN_REVIEW",
                recommendation_reasons=("risk",),
                recommendation_id="REC1",
            )
        ]

    def get_review(self, review_item_id):
        return (
            self.list_reviews()[0]
            if review_item_id == "R1"
            else None
        )

    def list_escalations(self):
        return self.list_reviews()

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from app.application.health.dependency_checks import (
    AsyncDependencyCheck,
    ConfigurationCheck,
)
from app.application.health.health_result import (
    HealthStatus,
)
from app.application.health.operational_dashboard_service import (
    OperationalDashboardService,
)
from app.application.health.operational_event import (
    OperationalEvent,
    OperationalEventType,
    OperationalSeverity,
    OperationalSurface,
)
from app.application.health.operational_event_service import (
    OperationalEventService,
)
from app.application.health.operational_health_snapshot import (
    OperationalHealthSnapshotService,
)
from app.application.health.production_readiness_service import (
    ProductionReadinessService,
)


async def healthy_probe():
    return True


def make_readiness():
    return ProductionReadinessService(
        checks=(
            ConfigurationCheck(
                name="telegram",
                configured=True,
            ),
            AsyncDependencyCheck(
                name="postgresql",
                probe=healthy_probe,
            ),
            AsyncDependencyCheck(
                name="llm_provider",
                probe=healthy_probe,
            ),
        )
    )


def make_service():
    health_service = (
        OperationalHealthSnapshotService(
            readiness_service=make_readiness()
        )
    )

    event_service = (
        OperationalEventService()
    )

    return (
        OperationalDashboardService(
            health_snapshot_service=(
                health_service
            ),
            event_service=event_service,
        ),
        event_service,
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_ws21_10_produces_complete_dashboard_contract():

    service, _events = make_service()

    snapshot = (
        await service.create_dashboard_snapshot()
    )

    payload = snapshot.as_dict()

    assert "generated_at" in payload
    assert "overall" in payload
    assert "private_classroom" in payload
    assert "announcement_group" in payload
    assert "incidents" in payload

    assert (
        payload["overall"]["status"]
        == "healthy"
    )

    assert (
        payload["overall"]["health_score"]
        == 100
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_ws21_10_private_classroom_contains_dependencies():

    service, _events = make_service()

    snapshot = (
        await service.create_dashboard_snapshot()
    )

    private = (
        snapshot.private_classroom
    )

    dependency_names = {
        dependency.name
        for dependency
        in private.dependencies
    }

    assert "telegram" in dependency_names
    assert "postgresql" in dependency_names
    assert "llm_provider" in dependency_names


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_ws21_10_announcement_group_is_separate():

    service, event_service = (
        make_service()
    )

    event_service.record_event(
        OperationalEvent(
            event_type=(
                OperationalEventType
                .NOTIFICATION_FAILURE
            ),
            surface=(
                OperationalSurface
                .ANNOUNCEMENT_GROUP
            ),
            correlation_id="announcement-001",
            severity=(
                OperationalSeverity.HIGH
            ),
        )
    )

    snapshot = (
        await service.create_dashboard_snapshot()
    )

    assert (
        snapshot.announcement_group
        .surface
        is OperationalSurface.ANNOUNCEMENT_GROUP
    )

    assert (
        snapshot.announcement_group
        .incidents.total
        == 1
    )

    assert (
        snapshot.private_classroom
        .incidents.total
        == 0
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_ws21_10_private_operational_metrics_are_exposed():

    service, event_service = (
        make_service()
    )

    event_service.record_event(
        OperationalEvent(
            event_type=(
                OperationalEventType.MESSAGE_FAILURE
            ),
            surface=(
                OperationalSurface.PRIVATE_CLASSROOM
            ),
            correlation_id="message-001",
        )
    )

    event_service.record_event(
        OperationalEvent(
            event_type=(
                OperationalEventType.RATE_LIMIT
            ),
            surface=(
                OperationalSurface.PRIVATE_CLASSROOM
            ),
            correlation_id="rate-001",
        )
    )

    event_service.record_event(
        OperationalEvent(
            event_type=(
                OperationalEventType.ABUSE
            ),
            surface=(
                OperationalSurface.PRIVATE_CLASSROOM
            ),
            correlation_id="abuse-001",
        )
    )

    snapshot = (
        await service.create_dashboard_snapshot()
    )

    metrics = (
        snapshot.private_classroom
        .metrics
    )

    assert metrics.message_failures == 1
    assert metrics.rate_limit_events == 1
    assert metrics.abuse_events == 1


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_ws21_10_incident_severity_counts():

    service, event_service = (
        make_service()
    )

    event_service.record_event(
        OperationalEvent(
            event_type=(
                OperationalEventType
                .DATABASE_FAILURE
            ),
            surface=(
                OperationalSurface.PRIVATE_CLASSROOM
            ),
            correlation_id="critical-001",
            severity=(
                OperationalSeverity.CRITICAL
            ),
        )
    )

    event_service.record_event(
        OperationalEvent(
            event_type=(
                OperationalEventType.PROVIDER_FAILURE
            ),
            surface=(
                OperationalSurface.PRIVATE_CLASSROOM
            ),
            correlation_id="high-001",
            severity=(
                OperationalSeverity.HIGH
            ),
        )
    )

    snapshot = (
        await service.create_dashboard_snapshot()
    )

    summary = (
        snapshot.private_classroom
        .incidents
    )

    assert summary.total == 2
    assert summary.open == 2
    assert summary.critical == 1
    assert summary.high == 1


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_ws21_10_incident_includes_investigation_fields():

    service, event_service = (
        make_service()
    )

    event_service.record_event(
        OperationalEvent(
            event_type=(
                OperationalEventType.MESSAGE_FAILURE
            ),
            surface=(
                OperationalSurface.PRIVATE_CLASSROOM
            ),
            correlation_id="investigate-001",
            severity=(
                OperationalSeverity.HIGH
            ),
            user_reference="user-ref-001",
            world_id="world-fr",
        )
    )

    snapshot = (
        await service.create_dashboard_snapshot()
    )

    assert len(
        snapshot.incidents
    ) == 1

    incident = snapshot.incidents[0]

    assert (
        incident.correlation_id
        == "investigate-001"
    )

    assert (
        incident.user_reference
        == "user-ref-001"
    )

    assert (
        incident.world_id
        == "world-fr"
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_ws21_10_dashboard_contract_exposes_no_learner_content():

    service, event_service = (
        make_service()
    )

    event_service.record_event(
        OperationalEvent(
            event_type=(
                OperationalEventType.MESSAGE_FAILURE
            ),
            surface=(
                OperationalSurface.PRIVATE_CLASSROOM
            ),
            correlation_id="safe-001",
            metadata={
                "message": (
                    "PRIVATE LEARNER CONTENT"
                ),
                "prompt": (
                    "PRIVATE PROMPT"
                ),
                "response": (
                    "PRIVATE AI RESPONSE"
                ),
                "safe_status": "timeout",
            },
        )
    )

    snapshot = (
        await service.create_dashboard_snapshot()
    )

    payload = str(
        snapshot.as_dict()
    )

    assert (
        "PRIVATE LEARNER CONTENT"
        not in payload
    )

    assert (
        "PRIVATE PROMPT"
        not in payload
    )

    assert (
        "PRIVATE AI RESPONSE"
        not in payload
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_ws21_10_dashboard_contract_contains_no_secrets():

    service, event_service = (
        make_service()
    )

    event_service.record_event(
        OperationalEvent(
            event_type=(
                OperationalEventType
                .PROVIDER_FAILURE
            ),
            surface=(
                OperationalSurface.PRIVATE_CLASSROOM
            ),
            correlation_id="secret-001",
            metadata={
                "api_key": "SUPER-SECRET",
                "password": "PASSWORD-SECRET",
                "database_url": (
                    "postgresql://secret"
                ),
            },
        )
    )

    snapshot = (
        await service.create_dashboard_snapshot()
    )

    payload = str(
        snapshot.as_dict()
    ).lower()

    assert "super-secret" not in payload
    assert "password-secret" not in payload
    assert "postgresql://secret" not in payload

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_ws21_10_dashboard_snapshot_is_read_only():

    service, _events = make_service()

    first = (
        await service.create_dashboard_snapshot()
    )

    second = (
        await service.create_dashboard_snapshot()
    )

    assert (
        first.private_classroom
        .incidents.total
        == second.private_classroom
        .incidents.total
    )

    assert (
        first.announcement_group
        .incidents.total
        == second.announcement_group
        .incidents.total
    )

    service, _events = make_service()

    snapshot = (
        await service.create_dashboard_snapshot()
    )

    payload = snapshot.as_dict()

    assert isinstance(payload, dict)

    assert isinstance(
        payload["incidents"],
        list,
    )

    assert isinstance(
        payload["private_classroom"],
        dict,
    )

    assert isinstance(
        payload["announcement_group"],
        dict,
    )

    assert isinstance(
        payload["overall"],
        dict,
    )

from __future__ import annotations

import os
from uuid import uuid4

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import text

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery_audit import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus,
)
from app.infrastructure.operations_center.sqlalchemy_platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery_repository import (
    SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.operations_center.sqlalchemy_platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery_audit_repository import (
    SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.database import (
    create_async_database_engine,
    create_async_session_factory,
)

pytestmark = pytest.mark.asyncio


def required():
    if not os.getenv("DATABASE_URL"):
        pytest.skip("DATABASE_URL is required for PostgreSQL integration tests.")


async def test_delivery_lifecycle_is_durably_audited():
    required()
    engine = create_async_database_engine()
    key = f"pi-audit-lifecycle-{uuid4()}"

    try:
        factory = create_async_session_factory(engine)
        delivery = SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRepository(factory)
        audit = SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRepository(factory)

        await delivery.create_started(
            notification_key=key,
            recommendation_id="REC-AUDIT",
            risk_decision_id="DEC-AUDIT",
            review_item_id="REV-AUDIT",
            correlation_id="CORR-AUDIT",
        )
        await delivery.mark_failed(
            notification_key=key,
            error_code="PROVIDER_ERROR",
        )
        await delivery.start_retry(notification_key=key)
        await delivery.mark_succeeded(
            notification_key=key,
            provider_message_id="provider-audit-2",
        )

        events = await audit.list_by_notification_key(key)

        assert [event.status for event in events] == [
            PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus.STARTED,
            PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus.FAILED,
            PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus.STARTED,
            PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus.SUCCEEDED,
        ]
        assert [event.attempt for event in events] == [1, 1, 2, 2]
        assert events[1].error_code == "PROVIDER_ERROR"
        assert events[-1].provider_message_id == "provider-audit-2"
    finally:
        async with create_async_session_factory(engine)() as session:
            await session.execute(
                text(
                    """
                    DELETE FROM platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery_audit
                    WHERE notification_key = :key
                    """
                ),
                {"key": key},
            )
            await session.execute(
                text(
                    """
                    DELETE FROM platform_intelligence_escalation_deliveries
                    WHERE notification_key = :key
                    """
                ),
                {"key": key},
            )
            await session.commit()
        await engine.dispose()

from __future__ import annotations

import os
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from uuid import uuid4

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import text

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus,
)
from app.infrastructure.operations_center.sqlalchemy_platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery_repository import (
    SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.database import (
    create_async_database_engine,
    create_async_session_factory,
)


pytestmark = pytest.mark.asyncio


def _database_required() -> None:
    if not os.getenv("DATABASE_URL"):
        pytest.skip("DATABASE_URL is required for PostgreSQL integration tests.")


async def test_get_missing_notification_returns_none() -> None:
    _database_required()

    engine = create_async_database_engine()
    try:
        repository = SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRepository(
            create_async_session_factory(engine)
        )

        assert await repository.get_by_notification_key(
            f"pi-test-missing-{uuid4()}"
        ) is None
    finally:
        await engine.dispose()


async def test_started_success_and_idempotent_record_round_trip() -> None:
    _database_required()

    engine = create_async_database_engine()
    key = f"pi-test-{uuid4()}"

    try:
        repository = SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRepository(
            create_async_session_factory(engine)
        )

        started = await repository.create_started(
            notification_key=key,
            recommendation_id="recommendation-test",
            risk_decision_id="decision-test",
            review_item_id="review-test",
            correlation_id="correlation-test",
        )

        assert started.status is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED
        assert started.attempt == 1

        succeeded = await repository.mark_succeeded(
            notification_key=key,
            provider_message_id="provider-message-1",
        )

        assert (
            succeeded.status
            is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.SUCCEEDED
        )
        assert succeeded.provider_message_id == "provider-message-1"
        assert succeeded.attempt == 1

        loaded = await repository.get_by_notification_key(key)

        assert loaded is not None
        assert (
            loaded.status
            is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.SUCCEEDED
        )
        assert loaded.provider_message_id == "provider-message-1"
    finally:
        await engine.dispose()


async def test_failed_delivery_can_retry_with_incremented_attempt() -> None:
    _database_required()

    engine = create_async_database_engine()
    key = f"pi-test-retry-{uuid4()}"

    try:
        repository = SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRepository(
            create_async_session_factory(engine)
        )

        await repository.create_started(
            notification_key=key,
            recommendation_id="recommendation-retry",
            risk_decision_id="decision-retry",
            review_item_id="review-retry",
            correlation_id="correlation-retry",
        )

        failed = await repository.mark_failed(
            notification_key=key,
            error_code="PROVIDER_ERROR",
        )

        assert failed.status is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED
        assert failed.attempt == 1
        assert failed.error_code == "PROVIDER_ERROR"

        retried = await repository.start_retry(
            notification_key=key,
        )

        assert retried.status is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED
        assert retried.attempt == 2
        assert retried.provider_message_id is None
        assert retried.error_code is None
    finally:
        await engine.dispose()


async def test_list_retry_candidates_returns_only_failed_and_started_in_deterministic_order() -> None:
    _database_required()

    engine = create_async_database_engine()
    suffix = uuid4()

    started_key = f"pi-test-candidate-started-{suffix}"
    failed_key = f"pi-test-candidate-failed-{suffix}"
    succeeded_key = f"pi-test-candidate-succeeded-{suffix}"

    test_keys = (
        started_key,
        failed_key,
        succeeded_key,
    )

    try:
        repository = SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRepository(
            create_async_session_factory(engine)
        )

        await repository.create_started(
            notification_key=started_key,
            recommendation_id=f"recommendation-started-{suffix}",
            risk_decision_id=f"decision-started-{suffix}",
            review_item_id=f"review-started-{suffix}",
            correlation_id=f"correlation-started-{suffix}",
        )

        await repository.create_started(
            notification_key=failed_key,
            recommendation_id=f"recommendation-failed-{suffix}",
            risk_decision_id=f"decision-failed-{suffix}",
            review_item_id=f"review-failed-{suffix}",
            correlation_id=f"correlation-failed-{suffix}",
        )

        await repository.mark_failed(
            notification_key=failed_key,
            error_code="PROVIDER_ERROR",
        )

        await repository.create_started(
            notification_key=succeeded_key,
            recommendation_id=f"recommendation-succeeded-{suffix}",
            risk_decision_id=f"decision-succeeded-{suffix}",
            review_item_id=f"review-succeeded-{suffix}",
            correlation_id=f"correlation-succeeded-{suffix}",
        )

        await repository.mark_succeeded(
            notification_key=succeeded_key,
            provider_message_id="provider-message-succeeded",
        )

        # Establish a deterministic ordering for this test's records.
        #
        # The production repository deliberately returns the global retry
        # candidate set. This test therefore retrieves a sufficiently large
        # bounded batch and then isolates its own records for assertions.
        older = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(minutes=5)
        newer = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(minutes=1)

        async with repository._session_factory() as session:  # noqa: SLF001
            await session.execute(
                text(
                    """
                    UPDATE platform_intelligence_escalation_deliveries
                    SET updated_at = :updated_at
                    WHERE notification_key = :notification_key
                    """
                ),
                {
                    "updated_at": older,
                    "notification_key": failed_key,
                },
            )

            await session.execute(
                text(
                    """
                    UPDATE platform_intelligence_escalation_deliveries
                    SET updated_at = :updated_at
                    WHERE notification_key = :notification_key
                    """
                ),
                {
                    "updated_at": newer,
                    "notification_key": started_key,
                },
            )

            await session.commit()

        # The table is shared across integration-test runs, so previous
        # FAILED/STARTED records may legitimately exist. Do not let those
        # unrelated records consume the small production-style limit used
        # by the test.
        candidates = await repository.list_retry_candidates(
            limit=1000,
        )

        candidate_by_key = {
            record.notification_key: record
            for record in candidates
            if record.notification_key in test_keys
        }

        assert failed_key in candidate_by_key
        assert started_key in candidate_by_key
        assert succeeded_key not in candidate_by_key

        own_candidate_keys = [
            record.notification_key
            for record in candidates
            if record.notification_key in test_keys
        ]

        assert own_candidate_keys == [
            failed_key,
            started_key,
        ]

        assert all(
            record.status
            in (
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED,
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED,
            )
            for record in (
                candidate_by_key[failed_key],
                candidate_by_key[started_key],
            )
        )
    finally:
        # Clean up only this test's own records.
        async with repository._session_factory() as session:  # noqa: SLF001
            await session.execute(
                text(
                    """
                    DELETE FROM platform_intelligence_escalation_deliveries
                    WHERE notification_key IN (
                        :started_key,
                        :failed_key,
                        :succeeded_key
                    )
                    """
                ),
                {
                    "started_key": started_key,
                    "failed_key": failed_key,
                    "succeeded_key": succeeded_key,
                },
            )
            await session.commit()

        await engine.dispose()


async def test_list_retry_candidates_respects_limit() -> None:
    _database_required()

    engine = create_async_database_engine()
    suffix = uuid4()

    keys = [
        f"pi-test-candidate-limit-{suffix}-{index}"
        for index in range(3)
    ]

    try:
        repository = SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRepository(
            create_async_session_factory(engine)
        )

        for index, key in enumerate(keys):
            await repository.create_started(
                notification_key=key,
                recommendation_id=f"recommendation-limit-{suffix}-{index}",
                risk_decision_id=f"decision-limit-{suffix}-{index}",
                review_item_id=f"review-limit-{suffix}-{index}",
                correlation_id=f"correlation-limit-{suffix}-{index}",
            )

            await repository.mark_failed(
                notification_key=key,
                error_code="PROVIDER_ERROR",
            )

        # Use a sufficiently large query window so unrelated historical
        # integration-test rows cannot prevent this test's own records from
        # being discovered.
        candidates = await repository.list_retry_candidates(
            limit=1000,
        )

        own_candidates = [
            record
            for record in candidates
            if record.notification_key in keys
        ]

        assert len(own_candidates) == 3

        # Validate the actual repository limit independently.
        limited_candidates = await repository.list_retry_candidates(
            limit=2,
        )

        assert len(limited_candidates) <= 2
    finally:
        async with repository._session_factory() as session:  # noqa: SLF001
            await session.execute(
                text(
                    """
                    DELETE FROM platform_intelligence_escalation_deliveries
                    WHERE notification_key IN (
                        :key_0,
                        :key_1,
                        :key_2
                    )
                    """
                ),
                {
                    "key_0": keys[0],
                    "key_1": keys[1],
                    "key_2": keys[2],
                },
            )
            await session.commit()

        await engine.dispose()
