from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import os
from uuid import uuid4

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_dispatch import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchService,
)
from app.infrastructure.operations_center.sqlalchemy_platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery_repository import (
    SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.database import (
    create_async_database_engine,
    create_async_session_factory,
)


pytestmark = pytest.mark.asyncio


def _database_required() -> None:
    if not os.getenv("DATABASE_URL"):
        pytest.skip("DATABASE_URL is required for PostgreSQL integration tests.")


class FakeEmailWorkflow:
    """Deterministic email-workflow double; no external provider is called."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        provider_message_id: str = "integration-provider-message-1",
        error: Exception | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.calls: list[object] = []
        self.provider_message_id = provider_message_id
        self.error = error

    async def send(self, request: object) -> dict[str, str]:
        self.calls.append(request)

        if self.error is not None:
            raise self.error

        return {
            "status": "accepted",
            "provider_message_id": self.provider_message_id,
        }


def _make_request(
    recommendation_id: str,
    *,
    correlation_id: str | None = None,
) -> PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest:
    return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest(
        recommendation_id=recommendation_id,
        risk_decision_id=f"risk-{recommendation_id}",
        review_item_id=f"review-{recommendation_id}",
        risk_level="HIGH",
        risk_score=0.82,
        quality_gate_status="PASSED",
        review_status="IN_REVIEW",
        decision_status="PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW",
        notification_key=(
            "platform_intelligence_escalation:"
            f"{recommendation_id}"
        ),
        correlation_id=correlation_id or f"correlation-{recommendation_id}",
        route="RISK_OWNER",
        reasons=("High risk level.",),
    )


def _new_repository(engine):
    return SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRepository(
        create_async_session_factory(engine)
    )


async def _dispose(engine) -> None:
    await engine.dispose()


async def test_dispatch_persists_success_and_replays_after_new_service_instance() -> None:
    _database_required()

    recommendation_id = f"pi-e2e-replay-{uuid4()}"
    engine_one = create_async_database_engine()

    try:
        repository_one = _new_repository(engine_one)
        workflow_one = FakeEmailWorkflow(
            provider_message_id="provider-message-restart-safe",
        )
        service_one = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchService(
            delivery_repository=repository_one,
            email_workflow=workflow_one,
        )

        first = await service_one.dispatch(
            request=_make_request(recommendation_id),
            email_request=object(),
        )

        assert first.status.value == "SENT"
        assert first.provider_message_id == "provider-message-restart-safe"
        assert len(workflow_one.calls) == 1

        persisted = await repository_one.get_by_notification_key(
            first.notification_key
        )
        assert persisted is not None
        assert persisted.status is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.SUCCEEDED
        assert persisted.attempt == 1
        assert persisted.provider_message_id == "provider-message-restart-safe"
    finally:
        await _dispose(engine_one)

    # Simulate a new application/repository instance after restart.
    engine_two = create_async_database_engine()

    try:
        repository_two = _new_repository(engine_two)
        workflow_two = FakeEmailWorkflow(
            provider_message_id="must-not-be-used",
        )
        service_two = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchService(
            delivery_repository=repository_two,
            email_workflow=workflow_two,
        )

        second = await service_two.dispatch(
            request=_make_request(recommendation_id),
            email_request=object(),
        )

        assert second.status.value == "IDEMPOTENT_REPLAY"
        assert second.provider_message_id == "provider-message-restart-safe"
        assert second.attempt == 1
        assert workflow_two.calls == []

        persisted_after_restart = (
            await repository_two.get_by_notification_key(
                second.notification_key
            )
        )
        assert persisted_after_restart is not None
        assert (
            persisted_after_restart.status
            is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.SUCCEEDED
        )
        assert persisted_after_restart.attempt == 1
        assert (
            persisted_after_restart.provider_message_id
            == "provider-message-restart-safe"
        )
    finally:
        await _dispose(engine_two)


async def test_started_delivery_is_retried_after_service_restart() -> None:
    _database_required()

    recommendation_id = f"pi-e2e-started-retry-{uuid4()}"
    request = _make_request(recommendation_id)
    engine_one = create_async_database_engine()

    try:
        repository_one = _new_repository(engine_one)

        started = await repository_one.create_started(
            notification_key=request.notification_key,
            recommendation_id=request.recommendation_id,
            risk_decision_id=request.risk_decision_id,
            review_item_id=request.review_item_id,
            correlation_id=request.correlation_id,
        )

        assert started.status is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED
        assert started.attempt == 1
    finally:
        await _dispose(engine_one)

    # The STARTED record represents an interrupted attempt. A new service
    # instance must recover it and complete delivery without creating a row.
    engine_two = create_async_database_engine()

    try:
        repository_two = _new_repository(engine_two)
        workflow_two = FakeEmailWorkflow(
            provider_message_id="provider-message-recovered",
        )
        service_two = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchService(
            delivery_repository=repository_two,
            email_workflow=workflow_two,
        )

        result = await service_two.dispatch(
            request=request,
            email_request=object(),
        )

        assert result.status.value == "SENT"
        assert result.provider_message_id == "provider-message-recovered"
        assert result.attempt == 1
        assert len(workflow_two.calls) == 1

        persisted = await repository_two.get_by_notification_key(
            request.notification_key
        )
        assert persisted is not None
        assert persisted.status is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.SUCCEEDED
        assert persisted.attempt == 1
        assert persisted.provider_message_id == "provider-message-recovered"
    finally:
        await _dispose(engine_two)


async def test_duplicate_notification_key_is_protected_by_postgresql() -> None:
    _database_required()

    recommendation_id = f"pi-e2e-duplicate-{uuid4()}"
    notification_key = (
        "platform_intelligence_escalation:"
        f"{recommendation_id}"
    )
    engine = create_async_database_engine()

    async def create(repository):
        return await repository.create_started(
            notification_key=notification_key,
            recommendation_id=recommendation_id,
            risk_decision_id=f"risk-{recommendation_id}",
            review_item_id=f"review-{recommendation_id}",
            correlation_id=f"correlation-{recommendation_id}",
        )

    try:
        repository_one = _new_repository(engine)
        repository_two = _new_repository(engine)

        results = await asyncio.gather(
            create(repository_one),
            create(repository_two),
            return_exceptions=True,
        )

        successes = [result for result in results if not isinstance(result, Exception)]
        failures = [result for result in results if isinstance(result, Exception)]

        assert len(successes) == 1
        assert len(failures) == 1

        persisted = await repository_one.get_by_notification_key(
            notification_key
        )
        assert persisted is not None
        assert persisted.status is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED
        assert persisted.attempt == 1
    finally:
        await _dispose(engine)

from __future__ import annotations

import os
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from uuid import uuid4

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import text

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus,
)
from app.infrastructure.operations_center.sqlalchemy_platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery_repository import (
    SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.database import (
    create_async_database_engine,
    create_async_session_factory,
)


pytestmark = pytest.mark.asyncio


def _database_required() -> None:
    if not os.getenv("DATABASE_URL"):
        pytest.skip("DATABASE_URL is required for PostgreSQL integration tests.")


async def test_claim_retry_candidate_is_atomic_and_reclaimable_after_lease_expiry() -> None:
    _database_required()

    engine = create_async_database_engine()
    key = f"pi-claim-{uuid4()}"

    try:
        repository = SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRepository(
            create_async_session_factory(engine)
        )

        await repository.create_started(
            notification_key=key,
            recommendation_id=f"recommendation-{uuid4()}",
            risk_decision_id=f"decision-{uuid4()}",
            review_item_id=f"review-{uuid4()}",
            correlation_id=f"correlation-{uuid4()}",
        )
        await repository.mark_failed(
            notification_key=key,
            error_code="PROVIDER_ERROR",
        )

        first_token = str(uuid4())
        first_claimed_at = datetime(2026, 9, 22, 12, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        first_expiry = first_claimed_at + timedelta(minutes=5)

        first = await repository.claim_retry_candidate(
            notification_key=key,
            claim_token=first_token,
            claimed_at=first_claimed_at,
            lease_expires_at=first_expiry,
        )
        assert first is True

        second = await repository.claim_retry_candidate(
            notification_key=key,
            claim_token=str(uuid4()),
            claimed_at=first_claimed_at + timedelta(seconds=1),
            lease_expires_at=first_expiry + timedelta(seconds=1),
        )
        assert second is False

        third_token = str(uuid4())
        third_claimed_at = first_expiry + timedelta(seconds=1)
        third = await repository.claim_retry_candidate(
            notification_key=key,
            claim_token=third_token,
            claimed_at=third_claimed_at,
            lease_expires_at=third_claimed_at + timedelta(minutes=5),
        )
        assert third is True

        async with repository._session_factory() as session:  # noqa: SLF001
            row = (
                await session.execute(
                    text(
                        """
                        SELECT claim_token, claimed_at, lease_expires_at
                        FROM platform_intelligence_escalation_deliveries
                        WHERE notification_key = :notification_key
                        """
                    ),
                    {"notification_key": key},
                )
            ).mappings().one()

        assert row["claim_token"] == third_token
        assert row["claimed_at"] == third_claimed_at
    finally:
        async with repository._session_factory() as session:  # noqa: SLF001
            await session.execute(
                text(
                    """
                    DELETE FROM platform_intelligence_escalation_deliveries
                    WHERE notification_key = :notification_key
                    """
                ),
                {"notification_key": key},
            )
            await session.commit()
        await engine.dispose()


async def test_claim_does_not_claim_succeeded_delivery() -> None:
    _database_required()

    engine = create_async_database_engine()
    key = f"pi-claim-success-{uuid4()}"

    try:
        repository = SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRepository(
            create_async_session_factory(engine)
        )

        await repository.create_started(
            notification_key=key,
            recommendation_id=f"recommendation-{uuid4()}",
            risk_decision_id=f"decision-{uuid4()}",
            review_item_id=f"review-{uuid4()}",
            correlation_id=f"correlation-{uuid4()}",
        )
        await repository.mark_succeeded(
            notification_key=key,
            provider_message_id="provider-message",
        )

        claimed = await repository.claim_retry_candidate(
            notification_key=key,
            claim_token=str(uuid4()),
            claimed_at=datetime(2026, 9, 22, 12, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
            lease_expires_at=datetime(
                2026, 9, 22, 12, 5, tzinfo=timezone.utc
            ),
        )

        assert claimed is False
    finally:
        async with repository._session_factory() as session:  # noqa: SLF001
            await session.execute(
                text(
                    """
                    DELETE FROM platform_intelligence_escalation_deliveries
                    WHERE notification_key = :notification_key
                    """
                ),
                {"notification_key": key},
            )
            await session.commit()
        await engine.dispose()

from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from app.api.dependencies.authorization import require_permission
from app.api.dependencies.identity import get_current_principal
from app.api.operations_center_email_workflows import (
    create_email_workflows_router,
)
from app.domain.authorization.permissions import Permission
from app.domain.identity.principal import AccountStatus, Principal


class FakeScalarResult:
    """Minimal scalar result returned by the fake SQLAlchemy session."""

    def all(self):
        return []


class FakeSession:
    """
    Minimal read-only SQLAlchemy Session contract required by the
    PostgreSQL email workflow registry.
    """

    def __enter__(self):
        return self

    def __exit__(self, exc_type, exc_value, traceback):
        return False

    def scalars(self, _statement):
        return FakeScalarResult()


class FakeSessionFactory:
    """Deterministic session factory for authorization tests."""

    def __call__(self):
        return FakeSession()


class FakeScheduler:
    """Minimal scheduler contract required by EmailWorkflowPortfolioService."""

    running = True

    def get_jobs(self):
        return []


class FakeSchedulerAdapter:
    scheduler = FakeScheduler()


class FakeSchedulerRuntime:
    scheduler_adapter = FakeSchedulerAdapter()


def make_principal(
    *,
    roles: frozenset[str],
) -> Principal:
    return Principal(
        user_id="email-workflow-authorization-test-user",
        email="operator@example.test",
        email_verified=True,
        account_status=AccountStatus.ACTIVE,
        roles=roles,
    )


def make_router():
    return create_email_workflows_router(
        session_factory=FakeSessionFactory(),
        operator_dependency=require_permission(
            Permission.INTERNAL_DATA_READ,
        ),
    )


def make_app(
    *,
    principal: Principal,
) -> FastAPI:
    application = FastAPI()

    application.state.scheduler_runtime = FakeSchedulerRuntime()

    application.include_router(
        make_router(),
    )

    # Override only the identity dependency.
    #
    # This preserves the real authorization path:
    #
    # get_current_principal
    #       -> require_permission(...)
    #       -> AuthorizationService
    #       -> PermissionDeniedError
    #       -> HTTP 403
    application.dependency_overrides[
        get_current_principal
    ] = lambda: principal

    return application


def test_beta_user_cannot_read_email_workflows():
    application = make_app(
        principal=make_principal(
            roles=frozenset({"beta_user"}),
        ),
    )

    with TestClient(application) as client:
        response = client.get(
            "/api/operations-center/email-workflows",
        )

    assert response.status_code == 403
    assert response.json() == {
        "detail": "Insufficient permission.",
    }


def test_operator_can_read_email_workflows():
    application = make_app(
        principal=make_principal(
            roles=frozenset({"operator"}),
        ),
    )

    with TestClient(application) as client:
        response = client.get(
            "/api/operations-center/email-workflows",
        )

    assert response.status_code == 200

    payload = response.json()

    assert payload["status"] == "ready"
    assert payload["count"] == 0
    assert payload["workflows"] == []


def test_admin_can_read_email_workflows():

def test_email_workflow_router_protects_collection_endpoint():
    router = make_router()

    routes = [
        route
        for route in router.routes
        if hasattr(route, "path")
        and route.path
        == "/api/operations-center/email-workflows"
    ]

    assert len(routes) == 1

    route = routes[0]

    assert route.methods == {"GET"}

    dependency_calls = {
        dependency.call
        for dependency in route.dependant.dependencies
    }

    assert len(dependency_calls) == 1

    permission_dependency = next(
        iter(dependency_calls)
    )

    assert permission_dependency is not None


def test_email_workflow_router_protects_item_endpoint():
    router = make_router()

    routes = [
        route
        for route in router.routes
        if hasattr(route, "path")
        and route.path
        == "/api/operations-center/email-workflows/{workflow_id}"
    ]

    assert len(routes) == 1

    route = routes[0]

    assert route.methods == {"GET"}

    dependency_calls = {
        dependency.call
        for dependency in route.dependant.dependencies
    }

    assert len(dependency_calls) == 1

    permission_dependency = next(
        iter(dependency_calls)
    )

    assert permission_dependency is not None

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_email import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationEmailRequest,
)
from app.infrastructure.email.platform_intelligence_escalation_email_sender import (
    ResendPlatformIntelligenceEscalationEmailSender,
)


class Provider:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.calls = []

    async def send(
        self,
        subject,
        content,
    ):
        self.calls.append(
            (
                subject,
                content,
            )
        )

        return {
            "status": "accepted",
            "message_id": "msg-1",
        }


class Manager:
    def __init__(self, provider):
        self.provider = provider

    def send(
        self,
        subject,
        content,
    ):
        return self.provider.send(
            subject,
            content,
        )


def make_request():
    return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationEmailRequest(
        recommendation_id="REC-1",
        risk_decision_id="DEC-1",
        review_item_id="REV-1",
        scope="operations-center",
        risk_level="HIGH",
        risk_score=0.8,
        quality_gate_status="PASSED",
        review_status="IN_REVIEW",
        decision_status="PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW",
        route="RISK_OWNER",
        reasons=("risk_level=HIGH",),
        evidence_references=("E1",),
        historical_references=("H1",),
        policy_id="P",
        policy_version="1",
        rationale="rationale",
        generated_at="2026-09-22T10:00:00+00:00",
        notification_key=(
            "platform_intelligence_escalation:REC-1"
        ),
    )


def test_sender_uses_route_recipient_and_shared_provider():
    provider = Provider()

    sender = ResendPlatformIntelligenceEscalationEmailSender(
        provider_manager=Manager(provider),
        recipients={
            "RISK_OWNER": "risk-owner@example.test",
        },
    )

    result = sender.send(
        make_request(),
        subject="subject",
        body="body",
        html_body="<p>body</p>",
    )

    asyncio.run(result)

    subject, content = provider.calls[0]

    assert subject == "subject"
    assert (
        content["recipient"]
        == "risk-owner@example.test"
    )
    assert (
        content["email_type"]
        == "platform_intelligence_escalation"
    )
    assert (
        content["recommendation_id"]
        == "REC-1"
    )

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from uuid import uuid4

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery_audit import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRecord,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus,
)


def common(status):
    return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRecord(
        audit_id=uuid4(),
        notification_key="platform_intelligence_escalation:REC-1",
        recommendation_id="REC-1",
        risk_decision_id="DEC-1",
        review_item_id="REV-1",
        correlation_id="CORR-1",
        attempt=1,
        status=status,
        occurred_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        provider_message_id="provider-1"
        if status is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus.SUCCEEDED
        else None,
        error_code="PROVIDER_ERROR"
        if status is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus.FAILED
        else None,
    )


def test_audit_record_is_immutable():
    record = common(PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus.STARTED)
    with pytest.raises((AttributeError, TypeError)):
        record.attempt = 2


def test_success_requires_provider_message_id():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="provider_message_id"):
        PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRecord(
            audit_id=uuid4(),
            notification_key="k",
            recommendation_id="r",
            risk_decision_id="d",
            review_item_id="v",
            correlation_id="c",
            attempt=1,
            status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus.SUCCEEDED,
            occurred_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        )


def test_failure_requires_error_code():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="error_code"):
        PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRecord(
            audit_id=uuid4(),
            notification_key="k",
            recommendation_id="r",
            risk_decision_id="d",
            review_item_id="v",
            correlation_id="c",
            attempt=1,
            status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus.FAILED,
            occurred_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        )
----- C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\tests\unit\operations_center\test_platform_intelligence_escalation_dispatch.py -----
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, replace

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_dispatch import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchService,
)


@dataclass
class DeliveryRecord:
    notification_key: str
    recommendation_id: str
    risk_decision_id: str
    review_item_id: str
    correlation_id: str
    attempt: int
    status: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus
    provider_message_id: str | None = None
    error_code: str | None = None


class FakeRepository:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.records: dict[str, DeliveryRecord] = {}
        self.created = 0
        self.retries = 0
        self.succeeded = 0
        self.failed = 0

    async def get_by_notification_key(
        self,
        notification_key: str,
    ) -> DeliveryRecord | None:
        return self.records.get(notification_key)

    async def create_started(self, **kwargs) -> DeliveryRecord:
        self.created += 1

        record = DeliveryRecord(
            **kwargs,
            attempt=1,
            status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED,
        )

        self.records[kwargs["notification_key"]] = record
        return record

    async def mark_succeeded(
        self,
        *,
        notification_key: str,
        provider_message_id: str | None,
    ) -> DeliveryRecord:
        self.succeeded += 1

        record = self.records[notification_key]
        record.status = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.SUCCEEDED
        record.provider_message_id = provider_message_id

        return record

    async def mark_failed(
        self,
        *,
        notification_key: str,
        error_code: str,
    ) -> DeliveryRecord:
        self.failed += 1

        record = self.records[notification_key]
        record.status = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED
        record.error_code = error_code

        return record

    async def start_retry(
        self,
        *,
        notification_key: str,
    ) -> DeliveryRecord:
        self.retries += 1

        record = self.records[notification_key]
        record.attempt += 1
        record.status = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED

        return record


@dataclass
class WorkflowResult:
    delivery: object


class FakeWorkflow:
    def __init__(
        self,
        result=None,
        error: Exception | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.calls: list[object] = []
        self.result = result or {
            "provider_message_id": "resend-123",
        }
        self.error = error

    def send(self, request: object) -> WorkflowResult:
        self.calls.append(request)

        if self.error:
            raise self.error

        return WorkflowResult(delivery=self.result)


def make_request(
    **overrides,
) -> PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest:
    values = {
        "recommendation_id": "rec-1",
        "risk_decision_id": "risk-1",
        "review_item_id": "review-1",
        "risk_level": "HIGH",
        "risk_score": 0.82,
        "quality_gate_status": "PASSED",
        "review_status": "IN_REVIEW",
        "decision_status": "PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW",
        "notification_key": (
            "platform_intelligence_escalation:rec-1"
        ),
        "correlation_id": "corr-1",
        "route": "RISK_OWNER",
        "reasons": ("High risk level.",),
    }

    values.update(overrides)

    return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest(**values)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dispatch_sends_and_persists_success():
    repo = FakeRepository()
    workflow = FakeWorkflow()

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchService(
        delivery_repository=repo,
        email_workflow=workflow,
    )

    result = await service.dispatch(
        request=make_request(),
        email_request=object(),
    )

    assert result.status.value == "SENT"
    assert result.provider_message_id == "resend-123"
    assert repo.created == 1
    assert repo.succeeded == 1
    assert len(workflow.calls) == 1


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_successful_delivery_is_terminal_idempotent_replay():
    repo = FakeRepository()
    workflow = FakeWorkflow()

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchService(
        delivery_repository=repo,
        email_workflow=workflow,
    )

    first = await service.dispatch(
        request=make_request(),
        email_request=object(),
    )

    second = await service.dispatch(
        request=make_request(),
        email_request=object(),
    )

    assert first.status.value == "SENT"
    assert second.status.value == "IDEMPOTENT_REPLAY"
    assert second.provider_message_id == "resend-123"
    assert len(workflow.calls) == 1


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_failed_delivery_is_retryable():
    repo = FakeRepository()

    failing = FakeWorkflow(
        error=RuntimeError("provider unavailable"),
    )

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchService(
        delivery_repository=repo,
        email_workflow=failing,
    )

    first = await service.dispatch(
        request=make_request(),
        email_request=object(),
    )

    assert first.status.value == "FAILED"
    assert repo.failed == 1

    succeeding = FakeWorkflow()

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchService(
        delivery_repository=repo,
        email_workflow=succeeding,
    )

    second = await service.dispatch(
        request=make_request(),
        email_request=object(),
    )

    assert second.status.value == "SENT"
    assert repo.retries == 1
    assert len(succeeding.calls) == 1


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_invalid_state_is_blocked_before_repository_or_email():
    repo = FakeRepository()
    workflow = FakeWorkflow()

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchService(
        delivery_repository=repo,
        email_workflow=workflow,
    )

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="quality gate"):
        await service.dispatch(
            request=make_request(
                quality_gate_status="FAILED",
            ),
            email_request=object(),
        )

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="review"):
        await service.dispatch(
            request=make_request(
                review_status="COMPLETED",
            ),
            email_request=object(),
        )

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="decision"):
        await service.dispatch(
            request=make_request(
                decision_status="ACCEPTED",
            ),
            email_request=object(),
        )

    assert repo.created == 0
    assert workflow.calls == []


def test_notification_key_is_recommendation_scoped():
    assert (
        PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchService.build_notification_key(
            "rec-7",
        )
        == "platform_intelligence_escalation:rec-7"
    )


def test_notification_key_must_match_recommendation():
    valid_request = make_request()

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="notification_key",
    ):
        replace(
            valid_request,
            notification_key=(
                "platform_intelligence_escalation:other"
            ),
        )

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicy,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyStatus,
)


REFERENCE = datetime(
    2026,
    9,
    22,
    10,
    0,
    tzinfo=timezone.utc,
)


@dataclass
class Record:
    notification_key: str
    recommendation_id: str
    risk_decision_id: str
    review_item_id: str
    correlation_id: str
    attempt: int
    status: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus
    provider_message_id: str | None = None
    error_code: str | None = None
    created_at: datetime | None = REFERENCE
    updated_at: datetime | None = REFERENCE


def policy(**overrides):
    values = {
        "policy_id": "PI-RETRY",
        "policy_version": "1.0",
        "status": (
            PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyStatus
            .APPROVED
        ),
        "max_attempts": 4,
        "base_backoff_seconds": 60,
        "max_backoff_seconds": 300,
        "retryable_error_codes": frozenset(
            {
                "TIMEOUT",
                "CONNECTION_ERROR",
                "PROVIDER_ERROR",
            }
        ),
        "retry_interrupted_started": True,
    }

    values.update(overrides)

    return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicy(
        **values
    )


def record(
    *,
    status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED,
    attempt=1,
    error_code="PROVIDER_ERROR",
):
    return Record(
        notification_key=(
            "platform_intelligence_escalation:REC-1"
        ),
        recommendation_id="REC-1",
        risk_decision_id="DEC-1",
        review_item_id="REV-1",
        correlation_id="CORR-1",
        attempt=attempt,
        status=status,
        error_code=error_code,
        created_at=REFERENCE,
        updated_at=REFERENCE,
    )


def test_retryable_failed_delivery_is_eligible_with_deterministic_backoff():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy()
    )

    result = service.evaluate(
        record=record(attempt=1),
        reference_at=REFERENCE + timedelta(seconds=60),
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision.ELIGIBLE
    )

    assert result.backoff_seconds == 60

    assert result.next_eligible_at == (
        REFERENCE + timedelta(seconds=60)
    )

    assert result.policy_id == "PI-RETRY"
    assert result.policy_version == "1.0"


def test_failed_delivery_is_not_due_before_backoff():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy()
    )

    result = service.evaluate(
        record=record(attempt=1),
        reference_at=REFERENCE + timedelta(seconds=59),
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision.ELIGIBLE
    )

    assert result.backoff_seconds == 60

    assert result.next_eligible_at == (
        REFERENCE + timedelta(seconds=60)
    )


def test_backoff_doubles_and_is_capped():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy()
    )

    assert service.backoff_seconds(attempt=1) == 60
    assert service.backoff_seconds(attempt=2) == 120
    assert service.backoff_seconds(attempt=3) == 240
    assert service.backoff_seconds(attempt=4) == 300
    assert service.backoff_seconds(attempt=10) == 300


def test_non_retryable_error_is_not_eligible():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy()
    )

    result = service.evaluate(
        record=record(
            error_code="INVALID_RECIPIENT"
        ),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision.NOT_ELIGIBLE
    )

    assert "not retryable" in result.reason


def test_missing_error_code_is_not_eligible():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy()
    )

    result = service.evaluate(
        record=record(error_code=None),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision.NOT_ELIGIBLE
    )

    assert "no error code" in result.reason


def test_maximum_attempts_is_enforced():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy()
    )

    result = service.evaluate(
        record=record(attempt=4),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision.NOT_ELIGIBLE
    )

    assert "Maximum delivery attempts" in result.reason


def test_draft_policy_cannot_authorize_retry():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy(
            status=(
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyStatus
                .DRAFT
            )
        )
    )

    result = service.evaluate(
        record=record(),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision.NOT_ELIGIBLE
    )

    assert "not APPROVED" in result.reason


def test_interrupted_started_delivery_is_policy_controlled():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy()
    )

    result = service.evaluate(
        record=record(
            status=(
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus
                .STARTED
            ),
            attempt=1,
            error_code=None,
        ),
        reference_at=REFERENCE + timedelta(seconds=60),
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision.ELIGIBLE
    )

    assert result.backoff_seconds == 60

    assert result.next_eligible_at == (
        REFERENCE + timedelta(seconds=60)
    )


def test_started_recovery_can_be_disabled_by_policy():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy(
            retry_interrupted_started=False
        )
    )

    result = service.evaluate(
        record=record(
            status=(
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus
                .STARTED
            ),
            error_code=None,
        ),
        reference_at=REFERENCE + timedelta(seconds=60),
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision.NOT_ELIGIBLE
    )


def test_succeeded_delivery_is_never_retryable():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy()
    )

    result = service.evaluate(
        record=record(
            status=(
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus
                .SUCCEEDED
            ),
            error_code=None,

        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision.NOT_ELIGIBLE
    )


def test_missing_delivery_timestamp_is_not_retryable():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy()
    )

    result = service.evaluate(
        record=Record(
            notification_key=(
                "platform_intelligence_escalation:REC-1"
            ),
            recommendation_id="REC-1",
            risk_decision_id="DEC-1",
            review_item_id="REV-1",
            correlation_id="CORR-1",
            attempt=1,
            status=(
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus
                .FAILED
            ),
            error_code="PROVIDER_ERROR",
            created_at=None,
            updated_at=None,
        ),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision.NOT_ELIGIBLE
    )

    assert "updated_at" in result.reason


def test_reference_time_must_be_timezone_aware():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy()
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="timezone-aware",
    ):
        service.evaluate(
            record=record(),
            reference_at=datetime(
                2026,
                9,
                22,
                10,
                0,
            ),
        )


def test_policy_rejects_invalid_backoff_configuration():
    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="max_backoff_seconds",
    ):
        policy(
            base_backoff_seconds=300,
            max_backoff_seconds=60,
        )


def test_policy_rejects_invalid_attempt_limit():
    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="max_attempts",
    ):
        policy(
            max_attempts=0
        )

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_candidate_discovery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateDiscoveryService,
)

T1 = datetime(2026, 9, 22, 10, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
T2 = datetime(2026, 9, 22, 10, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc)


@dataclass
class Record:
    notification_key: str
    recommendation_id: str
    risk_decision_id: str
    review_item_id: str
    correlation_id: str
    attempt: int
    status: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus
    provider_message_id: str | None = None
    error_code: str | None = None
    created_at: datetime | None = T1
    updated_at: datetime | None = T1


class Repository:
    def __init__(self, records):
        self.records = records
        self.limit = None

    async def list_retry_candidates(self, *, limit):
        self.limit = limit
        return self.records


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_discovery_returns_only_failed_and_started_candidates():
    repository = Repository(
        [
            Record(
                "key-succeeded",
                "rec-s",
                "dec-s",
                "rev-s",
                "corr-s",
                1,
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.SUCCEEDED,
                updated_at=T1,
            ),
            Record(
                "key-failed",
                "rec-f",
                "dec-f",
                "rev-f",
                "corr-f",
                1,
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED,
                error_code="PROVIDER_ERROR",
                updated_at=T2,
            ),
            Record(
                "key-started",
                "rec-t",
                "dec-t",
                "rev-t",
                "corr-t",
                1,
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED,
                updated_at=T1,
            ),
        ]
    )

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateDiscoveryService(
        repository=repository
    )

    result = await service.discover(limit=50)

    assert repository.limit == 50
    assert [item.notification_key for item in result.candidates] == [
        "key-started",
        "key-failed",
    ]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_discovery_is_deterministically_ordered():
    repository = Repository(
        [
            Record(
                "key-b",
                "rec-b",
                "dec-b",
                "rev-b",
                "corr-b",
                1,
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED,
                updated_at=T1,
            ),
            Record(
                "key-a",
                "rec-a",
                "dec-a",
                "rev-a",
                "corr-a",
                1,
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED,
                updated_at=T1,
            ),
        ]
    )

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateDiscoveryService(
        repository=repository
    )

    result = await service.discover(limit=10)

    assert [item.notification_key for item in result.candidates] == [
        "key-a",
        "key-b",
    ]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_discovery_preserves_retry_metadata():
    repository = Repository(
        [
            Record(
                "key-1",
                "rec-1",
                "dec-1",
                "rev-1",
                "corr-1",
                2,
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED,
                error_code="TIMEOUT",
                updated_at=T2,
            )
        ]
    )

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateDiscoveryService(
        repository=repository
    )

    result = await service.discover(limit=10)
    candidate = result.candidates[0]

    assert candidate.attempt == 2
    assert candidate.error_code == "TIMEOUT"
    assert candidate.updated_at == T2
    assert candidate.status == "FAILED"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_discovery_rejects_invalid_limit():
    repository = Repository([])

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateDiscoveryService(
        repository=repository
    )

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="at least 1"):
        await service.discover(limit=0)

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_claims import (
    RetryCandidateClaimStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_claim_service import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryClaimService,
)


class FakeClaimRepository:
    def __init__(self, result: bool) -> None:
        self.result = result
        self.calls = []

    async def claim_retry_candidate(self, **kwargs):
        self.calls.append(kwargs)
        return self.result


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_claim_returns_bounded_lease() -> None:
    repository = FakeClaimRepository(True)
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryClaimService(
        repository,
        lease_seconds=60,
    )
    reference = datetime(2026, 9, 22, 12, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)

    claim = await service.claim(
        notification_key="pi-key",
        claimed_at=reference,
    )

    assert claim is not None
    assert claim.notification_key == "pi-key"
    assert claim.claimed_at == reference
    assert claim.lease_expires_at == reference + timedelta(seconds=60)
    assert claim.status is RetryCandidateClaimStatus.CLAIMED
    assert repository.calls[0]["notification_key"] == "pi-key"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_claim_returns_none_when_candidate_is_already_claimed() -> None:
    repository = FakeClaimRepository(False)
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryClaimService(repository)

    claim = await service.claim(
        notification_key="pi-key",
        claimed_at=datetime(2026, 9, 22, 12, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
    )

    assert claim is None

----- C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\tests\unit\operations_center\test_platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_execution.py -----
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_dispatch import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchResult,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicy,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_execution import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionService,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus,
)


DELIVERY_TIME = datetime(
    2026,
    9,
    22,
    10,
    0,
    tzinfo=timezone.utc,
)

REFERENCE = datetime(
    2026,
    9,
    22,
    10,
    1,
    tzinfo=timezone.utc,
)


@dataclass
class Record:
    notification_key: str
    recommendation_id: str
    risk_decision_id: str
    review_item_id: str
    correlation_id: str
    attempt: int
    status: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus
    provider_message_id: str | None = None
    error_code: str | None = None
    created_at: datetime | None = DELIVERY_TIME
    updated_at: datetime | None = DELIVERY_TIME


class Repository:
    def __init__(self, record):
        self.record = record
        self.retries = 0

    async def get_by_notification_key(self, key):
        if self.record is None:
            return None

        if self.record.notification_key == key:
            return self.record

        return None

    async def start_retry(self, *, notification_key):
        self.retries += 1

        self.record.attempt += 1
        self.record.status = (
            PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED
        )
        self.record.error_code = None
        self.record.updated_at = REFERENCE

        return self.record


class Dispatch:
    def __init__(self):
        self.calls = 0

    async def dispatch(self, *, request, email_request):
        self.calls += 1

        return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchResult(
            status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchStatus.SENT,
            notification_key=request.notification_key,
            provider_message_id="provider-1",
            attempt=2,
        )


def policy():
    return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicy(
            policy_id="PI-RETRY",
            policy_version="1.0",
            status=(
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyStatus
                .APPROVED
            ),
            max_attempts=4,
            base_backoff_seconds=60,
            max_backoff_seconds=300,
            retryable_error_codes=frozenset(
                {"PROVIDER_ERROR"}
            ),
            retry_interrupted_started=True,
        )
    )


def request():
    return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest(
        recommendation_id="REC-1",
        risk_decision_id="DEC-1",
        review_item_id="REV-1",
        risk_level="HIGH",
        risk_score=0.82,
        quality_gate_status="PASSED",
        review_status="IN_REVIEW",
        decision_status="PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW",
        notification_key=(
            "platform_intelligence_escalation:REC-1"
        ),
        correlation_id="CORR-1",
        route="RISK_OWNER",
        reasons=("High risk.",),
    )


def record(
    status,
    attempt=1,
    error_code="PROVIDER_ERROR",
):
    return Record(
        notification_key=(
            "platform_intelligence_escalation:REC-1"
        ),
        recommendation_id="REC-1",
        risk_decision_id="DEC-1",
        review_item_id="REV-1",
        correlation_id="CORR-1",
        attempt=attempt,
        status=status,
        error_code=error_code,
        created_at=DELIVERY_TIME,
        updated_at=DELIVERY_TIME,
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_failed_delivery_retries_only_when_due():
    repository = Repository(
        record(
            PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED
        )
    )

    dispatch = Dispatch()

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionService(
        delivery_repository=repository,
        retry_policy=policy(),
        dispatch_service=dispatch,
    )

    early = await service.retry(
        request=request(),
        email_request=object(),
        reference_at=datetime(
            2026,
            9,
            22,
            10,
            0,
            30,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
    )

    assert (
        early.status
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus
        .NOT_DUE
    )

    assert repository.retries == 0
    assert dispatch.calls == 0

    due = await service.retry(
        request=request(),
        email_request=object(),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert (
        due.status
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus
        .RETRIED
    )

    assert repository.retries == 1
    assert dispatch.calls == 1

from dataclasses import dataclass

import pytest

    "platform_intelligence_escalation:recommendation-1"
)


def dispatch_request() -> PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest:
    return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest(
        recommendation_id="recommendation-1",
        risk_decision_id="decision-1",
        review_item_id="review-1",
        risk_level="HIGH",
        risk_score=0.82,
        quality_gate_status="PASSED",
        review_status="IN_REVIEW",
        decision_status="PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW",
        notification_key=NOTIFICATION_KEY,
        correlation_id="correlation-1",
        route="RISK_OWNER",
        reasons=("High risk level.",),
    )


def candidate() -> PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidate:
    return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidate(
        notification_key=NOTIFICATION_KEY,
        recommendation_id="recommendation-1",
        risk_decision_id="decision-1",
        review_item_id="review-1",
        correlation_id="correlation-1",
        attempt=1,
        status="FAILED",
        updated_at=REFERENCE,
        error_code="PROVIDER_ERROR",
    )


class FakePolicy:
    def __init__(self, eligible: bool) -> None:
        self.eligible = eligible
        self.calls = 0

    async def evaluate(self, **kwargs):
        self.calls += 1
        return self.eligible


class FakeClaimService:
    def __init__(self, claim) -> None:
        self.claim_result = claim
        self.calls = 0

    async def claim(self, **kwargs):
        self.calls += 1
        return self.claim_result


class FakeStateReader:
    def __init__(self, state) -> None:
        self.state = state
        self.calls = 0

    async def get_retry_state(self, **kwargs):
        self.calls += 1
        return self.state


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FakeClaim:
    token: str = "claim-token"


class FakeRetryExecution:
    def __init__(
        self,
        result: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionResult,
    ) -> None:
        self.result = result
        self.calls = 0
        self.kwargs = None

    async def retry(self, **kwargs):
        self.calls += 1
        self.kwargs = kwargs
        return self.result


def valid_state() -> PlatformIntelligenceRetryState:
    return PlatformIntelligenceRetryState(
        notification_key=NOTIFICATION_KEY,
        recommendation_id="recommendation-1",
        recommendation_status="RECOMMENDED",
        review_status="IN_REVIEW",
        decision_status="PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW",
        quality_gate_status="PASSED",
        dispatch_request=dispatch_request(),
        email_request=object(),
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_not_policy_eligible_does_not_claim_or_execute() -> None:
    policy = FakePolicy(False)
    claims = FakeClaimService(FakeClaim())
    state = FakeStateReader(valid_state())

    execution = FakeRetryExecution(
        PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionResult(
            status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus.RETRIED,
            notification_key=NOTIFICATION_KEY,
            attempt=1,
            reason="SUCCEEDED",
        )
    )

    orchestrator = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryOrchestrator(
        state_reader=state,
        policy_evaluator=policy,
        claim_service=claims,
        retry_execution=execution,
    )

    result = await orchestrator.run_candidate(
        candidate=candidate(),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert result.status is PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.SKIPPED
    assert result.reason == "RETRY_POLICY_NOT_ELIGIBLE"
    assert claims.calls == 0
    assert state.calls == 0
    assert execution.calls == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio

    claims = FakeClaimService(None)
    state = FakeStateReader(valid_state())

    execution = FakeRetryExecution(
        PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionResult(
            status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus.RETRIED,
            notification_key=NOTIFICATION_KEY,

            reason="SUCCEEDED",

    )

    assert result.status is PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.SKIPPED
    assert result.reason == "RETRY_CANDIDATE_ALREADY_CLAIMED"
    assert state.calls == 0
    assert execution.calls == 0

@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_valid_state_executes_after_claim() -> None:

    claims = FakeClaimService(FakeClaim())
    state = FakeStateReader(valid_state())

    execution = FakeRetryExecution(
        PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionResult(
            status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus.RETRIED,
            notification_key=NOTIFICATION_KEY,
            attempt=2,
            reason="RETRY_DISPATCHED",
            dispatch_result=type(
                "DispatchResult",
                (),
                {"provider_message_id": "provider-message-2"},
            )(),
        )
    )

    orchestrator = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryOrchestrator(
        state_reader=state,
        policy_evaluator=policy,
        claim_service=claims,
        retry_execution=execution,
    )

    result = await orchestrator.run_candidate(
        candidate=candidate(),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert result.status is PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.SUCCEEDED
    assert result.reason == "RETRY_DISPATCHED"
    assert result.attempt == 2
    assert result.provider_message_id == "provider-message-2"

    assert claims.calls == 1
    assert state.calls == 1
    assert execution.calls == 1

    assert execution.kwargs["request"] == dispatch_request()
    assert execution.kwargs["email_request"] is state.state.email_request
    assert execution.kwargs["reference_at"] == REFERENCE

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_candidates import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateBatch,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_orchestration import (
    PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunResult,
    PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_retry_worker import (
    PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemStatus,
    PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerRequest,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_retry_worker_service import (
    PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorker,
)


REFERENCE = datetime(2026, 9, 22, 12, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)


class Candidate:
    def __init__(self, key: str, attempt: int = 1) -> None:
        self.notification_key = key
        self.recommendation_id = f"rec-{key}"
        self.attempt = attempt


class Discovery:
    def __init__(self, candidates) -> None:
        self.candidates = tuple(candidates)
        self.calls = 0
        self.limit = None

    async def discover(self, *, limit: int):
        self.calls += 1
        self.limit = limit
        return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateBatch(
            candidates=self.candidates,
            limit=limit,
        )


class Orchestrator:
    def __init__(self, results=None, errors=None) -> None:
        self.results = results or {}
        self.errors = errors or {}
        self.calls = []

    async def run_candidate(self, *, candidate, reference_at):
        self.calls.append((candidate.notification_key, reference_at))
        if candidate.notification_key in self.errors:
            raise self.errors[candidate.notification_key]
        return self.results[candidate.notification_key]


def run_result(key, status, reason, attempt=1, provider=None):
    return PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunResult(
        notification_key=key,
        status=status,
        reason=reason,
        attempt=attempt,
        provider_message_id=provider,
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_empty_batch_is_a_noop():
    discovery = Discovery([])
    orchestrator = Orchestrator()

    worker = PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorker(
        discovery_service=discovery,
        orchestrator=orchestrator,
    )

    result = await worker.run(
        request=PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerRequest(limit=10),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert result.discovered_count == 0
    assert result.processed_count == 0
    assert result.succeeded_count == 0
    assert result.skipped_count == 0
    assert result.failed_count == 0
    assert result.items == ()
    assert discovery.calls == 1
    assert discovery.limit == 10
    assert orchestrator.calls == []


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_worker_processes_bounded_batch_and_preserves_results():
    candidates = [Candidate("key-a"), Candidate("key-b"), Candidate("key-c")]
    discovery = Discovery(candidates)
    orchestrator = Orchestrator(
        results={
            "key-a": run_result(
                "key-a",
                PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.SUCCEEDED,
                "RETRY_DISPATCHED",
                attempt=2,
                provider="provider-a",
            ),
            "key-b": run_result(
                "key-b",
                PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.SKIPPED,
                "RETRY_POLICY_NOT_ELIGIBLE",
            ),
            "key-c": run_result(
                "key-c",
                PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.FAILED,
                "DISPATCH_FAILED",
            ),
        }
    )

    worker = PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorker(
        discovery_service=discovery,
        orchestrator=orchestrator,
    )

    result = await worker.run(
        request=PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerRequest(limit=3),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert result.discovered_count == 3
    assert result.processed_count == 3
    assert result.succeeded_count == 1
    assert result.skipped_count == 1
    assert result.failed_count == 1
    assert [item.notification_key for item in result.items] == [
        "key-a",
        "key-b",
        "key-c",
    ]
    assert result.items[0].status is PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemStatus.SUCCEEDED
    assert result.items[0].provider_message_id == "provider-a"
    assert result.items[1].status is PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemStatus.SKIPPED
    assert result.items[2].status is PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemStatus.FAILED
    assert orchestrator.calls == [
        ("key-a", REFERENCE),
        ("key-b", REFERENCE),
        ("key-c", REFERENCE),
    ]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_one_candidate_exception_does_not_abort_remaining_candidates():
    candidates = [Candidate("key-a"), Candidate("key-b"), Candidate("key-c")]
    discovery = Discovery(candidates)
    orchestrator = Orchestrator(
        results={
            "key-a": run_result(
                "key-a",
                PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.SUCCEEDED,
                "RETRY_DISPATCHED",
            ),
            "key-c": run_result(
                "key-c",
                PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.SKIPPED,
                "RETRY_CANDIDATE_ALREADY_CLAIMED",
            ),
        },
        errors={"key-b": RuntimeError("provider failure")},
    )

    worker = PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorker(
        discovery_service=discovery,
        orchestrator=orchestrator,
    )

    result = await worker.run(
        request=PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerRequest(limit=3),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert result.processed_count == 3
    assert result.succeeded_count == 1
    assert result.skipped_count == 1
    assert result.failed_count == 1

    failed = result.items[1]
    assert failed.notification_key == "key-b"
    assert failed.reason == "CANDIDATE_EXECUTION_ERROR"
    assert failed.error_type == "RuntimeError"

    assert len(orchestrator.calls) == 3


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_worker_rejects_naive_reference_time():
    worker = PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorker(
        discovery_service=Discovery([]),
        orchestrator=Orchestrator(),
    )

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="timezone-aware"):
        await worker.run(
            request=PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerRequest(limit=1),
            reference_at=datetime(2026, 9, 22, 12, 0),
        )

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="at least 1"):
        PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerRequest(limit=0)


def test_worker_result_rejects_duplicate_notification_keys():
    from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_retry_worker import (
        PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemResult,
        PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerResult,
    )

    item = PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemResult(
        notification_key="duplicate",
        status=PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerItemStatus.SKIPPED,
        reason="SKIPPED",
        attempt=1,
    )

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="duplicate"):
        PlatformIntelligenceRetryWorkerResult(
            requested_limit=2,
            discovered_count=2,
            processed_count=2,
            succeeded_count=0,
            skipped_count=2,
            failed_count=0,
            items=(item, item),
        )

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_email import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationEmailRequest,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationEmailService,
)


class Sender:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.calls = []

    def send(
        self,
        request,
        *,
        subject,
        body,
        html_body,
    ):
        self.calls.append(
            (
                request,
                subject,
                body,
                html_body,
            )
        )
        return {
            "status": "accepted",
            "message_id": "msg-1",
        }


def make_request(**overrides):
    values = dict(
        recommendation_id="REC-1",
        risk_decision_id="DEC-1",
        review_item_id="REV-1",
        scope="operations-center",
        risk_level="HIGH",
        risk_score=0.82,
        quality_gate_status="PASSED",
        review_status="IN_REVIEW",
        decision_status="PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW",
        route="RISK_OWNER",
        reasons=(
            "risk_level=HIGH",
            "historical_recurrence_threshold_reached",
        ),
        evidence_references=("EVID-1",),
        historical_references=("HIST-1",),
        policy_id="PI-ESCALATION",
        policy_version="1.0",
        rationale=(
            "Controlled escalation recommended from governed evidence."
        ),
        generated_at="2026-09-22T10:00:00+00:00",
        notification_key=(
            "platform_intelligence_escalation:REC-1"
        ),
    )

    values.update(overrides)

    return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationEmailRequest(
        **values
    )


def test_build_email_contains_governed_traceability_and_control_language():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationEmailService(
        sender=Sender(),
        frontend_base_url="http://localhost:5173",
    )

    req = make_request()

    body = service.build_body(req)
    html = service.build_html(req)

    for value in (
        "REC-1",
        "DEC-1",
        "REV-1",
        "EVID-1",
        "HIST-1",
        "PI-ESCALATION",
        "human review",
    ):
        assert value.lower() in (
            body + html
        ).lower()

    assert "does not constitute" in body
    assert "/operations-center/compliance/reviews" in html
    assert "Notification key" not in body
    assert "Notification key" not in html
    assert "action required" in body.lower()
    assert "sla or response target" in body.lower()


def test_send_delegates_to_injected_sender():
    sender = Sender()

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationEmailService(
        sender=sender,
    )

    service.send(make_request())

    assert len(sender.calls) == 1
    assert sender.calls[0][0].recommendation_id == "REC-1"
    assert "HIGH" in sender.calls[0][1]


def test_non_review_state_is_rejected():
    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="pending human decision",
    ):
        make_request(
            decision_status="ACCEPTED"
        )


def test_failed_quality_gate_is_rejected():
    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="passed quality gate",
    ):
        make_request(
            quality_gate_status="FAILED"
        )
